### 5. Phase 41f — Personal Bookkeeping Integration (Xero v1)

- **Status:** 🟢 **CORE CLOSED** — 41f.0 ✅ **PR #690**; 41f.1 ✅ **PR #691**; 41f.2 ✅ **PR #692**; 41f.3 ✅ **PR #693**; 41f.4 ✅ **PR #697** (closed Phase 41f core); 41f.5 Monitrax Express **superseded by Phase 42** (Q-41F-1 closed 2026-05-07).
- **Started:** 2026-05-07
- **Owner:** Reza (vision/sign-off + 5 D-41F decisions ✅) + Claude (code)
- **Last touched:** 2026-05-07
- **Why this matters:** Closes the loop between the entity-aware tax engine (Phase 41e) and real-world entity bookkeeping. Today the user enters distributable-surplus / trust net income / SMSF balances by hand, with no audit trail back to source. After 41f, those numbers come from a stamped `EntityAccountingSnapshot` row with `sourceProvider + sourceTenantId + pulledAt`. **Strategic positioning: Monitrax CONSUMES Xero data, never replaces it.** Per §1.1 scope boundary in the spec doc, Phase 41f is **storage + understanding only** — no legal-document generation, no Xero write-back, no regulator-filing.

**Hard constraints (non-negotiable):**
- **Reuse Phase 32 OAuth + sync infrastructure** — no parallel auth surface; one canonical `AccountingIntegration` model with a scope discriminator.
- **Trust deed = 4-step confirm-before-apply** — never auto-apply legal-instrument interpretation. The friction is the feature.
- **HR-1/HR-2 preserved** — every imported number stamps `sourceProvider + sourceTenantId + pulledAt`; downstream tax engines consume from the typed snapshot row, never from raw Xero JSON.
- **Xero-only at v1** — MYOB + QuickBooks deferred to v2.
- **CDR posture: Xero data is NOT CDR-regulated** — handled as standard Privacy Act 1988 PII (encrypted-at-rest tokens; sanitized audit logs).

**Sub-PRs:**
- [x] **41f.0** — Design doc + Reza sign-off block — `docs/blueprint/PHASE_41F_BOOKKEEPING_INTEGRATION.md`. ✅ **APPROVED 2026-05-07** (D-41F-1 through D-41F-5 + §1.1 scope-boundary callout). **PR #690 MERGED.**
- [x] **41f.1** — Schema migration ✅ **PR #691 MERGED 2026-05-07**. Extends `AccountingIntegration` with scope discriminator + XOR CHECK constraint + two partial unique indexes per scope. New `entity_accounting_snapshots` + `trust_deed_extracted_rules` tables. New enums + back-relations. `prisma validate` ✅ + `prisma generate` ✅ + `tsc` ✅. CLAUDE.md §12.11 + §12.12 both satisfied.
- [x] **41f.2** — Xero OAuth + connect surface ✅ **PR #692 MERGED 2026-05-07**. New `lib/integrations/xero/` (zero new deps; `fetch`-based) with config + state (HMAC-signed stateless) + oauth (authorize URL + token exchange + refresh + revoke + tenant list; read-only scopes per §1.1) + index. Four new API routes (status / connect / disconnect / callback). New `/dashboard/entities/[id]/connect-bookkeeping` page with three states.
- [x] **41f.3** — Snapshot import pipeline + Div 7A surplus wiring ✅ **PR #693 MERGED 2026-05-07**. Three new `lib/integrations/xero/` modules (`tokenRefresh` / `reportParser` / `snapshotPuller`) + 2 API routes (`POST /refresh` / `GET /snapshots`) + `<SnapshotCard />` UI + Phase 41e.6 Div 7A classifier opt-in `Div7AClassifyOptions.distributableSurplus` extension. 29 new tests; 449 existing tax-engine tests still pass.

New `lib/integrations/trust-deed/` module (types + Zod + `pdfTextExtractor` via `unpdf` zero-new-deps + `geminiExtractor` strict structured output + `extractionService` lifecycle) + new `lib/services/trustDeedRulesService.ts` consumer-side reader + 3 API routes (GET/POST/PATCH/POST) + 4-step UI page + 36 new tests (88 integration total green).

New `lib/tax-engine/divisions/trustDeedValidation.ts` overlay + `MasterTaxPositionInput.confirmedDeedRulesByEntity` orchestrator input + new `getTrustDeedRules` AI advisor FACT_LOOKUP tool (registry size 10 → 11). Trust deeds are no longer decorative — extracted rules flow into both the tax engine + the AI advisor.

No standalone 41f.5 PR.

**5 strategic decisions ✅ APPROVED 2026-05-07 (PR #690):**

- **D-41F-2** SDK scope at v1 — Xero only; MYOB + QuickBooks → v2 ✅

**Risk:** Medium-low. Schema migration is additive + zero existing rows in `accounting_integrations` to migrate (verified pre-flight). Trust-deed parsing has the highest user-facing risk; 4-step flow + status lifecycle (`EXTRACTED → CONFIRMED | REJECTED`) is the structural defence. Xero rate-limiting mitigated via 24-hour `EntityAccountingSnapshot` cache + debounced refresh button.

**Blocking:** None — Phase 41f core CLOSED (PR #697). 41f.5 Monitrax Express formally superseded by Phase 42 (Q-41F-1 closed 2026-05-07).

### 6. Phase 41i.6 — Surface-Level Numerical Audit (the trustworthiness commitment)

- **Status:** 🟢 **CLOSED pending merge** — design doc ✅ **PR #694**; 41f.4 ✅ **PR #697** (Phase 41f core closed); 41i.6a ✅ **PR #699**; 41i.6b ✅ **PR #701**; 41i.6c runtime harness shipping (this PR — **CLOSES PHASE 41i.6**).
- **Started:** 2026-05-07
- **Owner:** Reza (vision/sign-off + 5 D-41i.6 decisions) + Claude (code)
- **Last touched:** 2026-05-07
- **Why this matters:** Reza brief 2026-05-07: *"I need the calc Audit agent to be able to review all relevant calculations of monitrax and the results... continuously make sure all calculations and produced numbers in monitrax is accurate and trustworthy and not some made up incorrect numbers."* This is the **trustworthiness commitment**. Phase 41i.0–5 catches calc-engine drift; 41i.3b (queued) catches per-user engine drift; 41i.6 catches the missing layer — surface-rendering drift (rendered tile ≠ canonical engine output). Together they form the comprehensive HR-3 safety net.

**Hard constraints (non-negotiable):**
- **Extend, don't fork** — reuse the existing `CalcAuditFinding` lifecycle. New enum value `L4_SURFACE_AUDIT` on `CalcAuditFindingSource`. Same admin queue, same alerting path.
- **HR-3 extended** — `PHASE_41_REGULATORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` invariant 11 now covers BOTH calc-engine drift AND surface-rendering drift. Reviewers reject any new financial surface without a registered `SurfaceDescriptor`.
- **Static + runtime, not just one** — 41i.6b CI lint catches the bug class at PR time (fast feedback); 41i.6c runtime harness catches data-dependent drift.
- **Top 10 surfaces at v1** — coverage scope is bounded; expand the descriptor list as workstream matures. Top 10: cashflow / expense / income / net-worth / health / tax / debt-freedom / emergency-fund / property-equity / investment-total.
- **Single admin queue** — don't split L1/L2/L3/L4 into separate pages. Filter chips per source.

**Sub-PRs:**
- [x] **41i.6 design doc** — `docs/blueprint/PHASE_41I_6_SURFACE_AUDIT.md` (this PR, awaiting Reza sign-off on 9 ticks in §12)
- [x] **41i.6a** — Surface descriptor registry + first 10 descriptors + `L4_SURFACE_AUDIT` enum migration (this PR, 2026-05-07). New `lib/calc-audit/surfaces/` module: `types.ts` (`SurfaceDescriptor` + `DiffTolerance` + `SurfaceValueKind` + `DEFAULT_TOLERANCE_BY_KIND` + `exceedsTolerance`) + `registry.ts` (singleton + `assertDescriptor` invariants) + 10 descriptors split across `descriptors/masterSnapshotDescriptors.ts` (6 master-direct) + `descriptors/cashflowDescriptors.ts` (4 via `quickMetrics`) + `index.ts` (auto-bootstrap). Schema migration `prisma/migrations/20260511100000_add_l4_surface_audit_source/`. 30 new tests. 191 total green. tsc clean. **All 10 v1 descriptors point at `lib/services/masterFinancialService.ts:getMasterFinancialSnapshot()`** — current components that bypass master will surface as L4 findings on first 41i.6c run.
- [x] **41i.6b** — CI static-analysis pass (this PR, 2026-05-07). New `scripts/lint-financial-surfaces.ts` detects 3 patterns: inline frequency conversion (`* 12 / * 52 / * 26 / * 4 / * 365` + reciprocals on financial-named identifiers); inline arithmetic between two financial fields; hardcoded financial constants adjacent to financial fields. Annotation: `// @financial-math-allowed: <reason>` (sidecar JSON tracks). **Baseline mechanism** — pre-existing 28 violations grandfathered so build doesn't break; only NEW violations fail. Stale baseline entries warn. Wired into `vercel-build` pre-build. 23 new tests. tsc clean.
- [x] **41i.6c** — Runtime audit harness + `[Full scan]` button (this PR, 2026-05-07). New `lib/calc-audit/surfaceAudit.ts` (`runSurfaceAuditForUser` + `runSurfaceAuditFullScan` async-generator + idempotent `recordSurfaceFinding` dedup). New `POST /api/admin/calc-audit/full-scan` with dual auth (admin session OR Cloud Scheduler shared secret) + NDJSON streaming. Admin UI extended with `[Full scan (L4)]` button + live progress card + source-filter chips. 12 new tests (7 outcome paths + 3 dedup + 2 multi-descriptor). 65 total surface-audit tests. tsc clean. **CLOSES PHASE 41i.6.**

**5 strategic decisions gated on Reza sign-off:**
- **D-41i.6-1** coverage scope at v1 — top 10 surfaces (cashflow / expense / income / net-worth / health / tax / debt-freedom / emergency-fund / property-equity / investment-total)
- **D-41i.6-2** diff tolerance — AUD 0 cents; ratios 0.01 (1bp); scores 0.5pt
- **D-41i.6-3** ship both static (41i.6b) AND runtime (41i.6c) — complementary
- **D-41i.6-4** manual `[Full scan]` button now; scheduled Cloud Scheduler full scan deferred to PROD-ready
- **D-41i.6-5** full canonical chain on every L4 finding (surfaceId / route / canonical source / canonical value / rendered value / diff / userId)

**Risk:** Medium. Static-analysis lint rules can produce false positives — exception annotations + sidecar JSON tracking mitigate. Runtime harness performance (full scan iterates every USER × every SURFACE) — initial limit to ~100 users for v1; optimise / index / parallelise at scale. UI streaming-progress complexity — NDJSON streaming is straightforward; add cancel affordance to prevent runaway scans.

**Blocking:** Reza sign-off on `PHASE_41I_6_SURFACE_AUDIT.md` §12 (9 ticks).

**Dependencies:** Phase 41i.0–5 ✅ (calc-audit foundation + persistent findings + alerting). Phase 41h ✅ (provides the structural-validator pattern that 41i.6 mirrors at the surface layer). 41f.4 should close before 41i.6a starts so the audit's first pass covers all of 41f's surfaces.

  - [x] **41E.5 — Wizard + docs consolidation** (PR open 2026-05-16): `acquisitionContractDate` + `isNewBuild` capture in `PropertiesStep` (conditional on post-cut-over `purchaseDate`) + `trustType` + `isForeignResident` selectors in `EntitiesStep` + bulk-create API plumbing for the new columns + round-trip activation tests (every flag false → true → false reversibility + flag independence) + `docs/architecture/03_DATA_MODEL.md` §O consolidated reference + new `docs/operational/UNCOMPUTED_REGISTER.md` (operator playbook for the 10 UC-* codes) + Phase doc status flipped 🟡 → 🟢 COMPLETE.

- **What's next (no urgency):**
  - **Stage 2** — per-measure rule mechanics, ~10-20 days spread across months as Treasury publishes drafts. M4 (foreign-resident CGT) + M5 (loss carry-back) are code-ready first; M1+M2 (neg gearing + CGT) wait on Treasury draft Q4 2026 / Q1 2027; M3 (trust 30%) ~Q1 2027.
  - **Stage 3** — Royal Assent flips: per-measure `commencementVerified` flag flip + knowledge-pack status bump from `bill` to `assented` + strip "announced — final law TBC" UI caveats. Each ~1 day.
  - [ ] **41E.5 — Tests + docs + UNCOMPUTED register**: round-trip activation tests + boundary fixtures + `docs/architecture/03_DATA_MODEL.md` consolidation + Phase doc status flips + IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN updates.
- **Started:** 2026-05-13 (Reza research brief)
- **Owner:** Reza (regulatory sign-off + staging order) + Claude (architecture + module skeletons)
- **Last touched:** 2026-05-16
- **Why this matters:** The 12 May 2026 Federal Budget introduced eight tax-law changes — three structural (negative gearing restricted to new builds; 50% CGT discount → cost-base indexation + 30% min tax rate; 30% min tax on discretionary-trust *taxable income*) plus foreign-resident CGT, loss refundability, foreign-purchase ban extension, VC caps, EV FBT phased transition, dynamic PAYG instalments. Monitrax's whole value proposition is *"your numbers, accurately, under the actual rules"* — botched reform implementation produces numbers users plan with that are wrong by tens of percent. Compounds with the AFSL-boundary architecture: the AI advisor's structuring-advice scenarios (`runStructuringScenario`, `runReformedCgtScenario`) become qualitatively more useful with the reform tools.

**Hard constraints (non-negotiable, all already locked in Phase 41 architecture):**
- **D-1** — full regulatory scope ships in the demo cut; no demo/PROD split for reform measures
- **D-2** — AFSL/TPB/NCCP boundary is structural via the closed `FACT_LOOKUP | SCENARIO_RUN` tool-kind discriminant in `lib/ai/tax-advisor/registry.ts`; reform tools inherit this — no `RECOMMENDATION` kind exists
- **HR-1** — every numeric claim in an AI response must trace to a tool result (validator-enforced in `lib/ai/tax-advisor/policy/validators.ts`); reform-aware scenarios produce numbers from the deterministic engine, never from the LLM
- **HR-2** — every authority citation must come from the knowledge pack, never AI memory; reform measures sit in `lib/ai/tax-advisor/knowledge/reform-2026-27.ts` (Stage 1) with `status: 'announced' | 'exposure-draft' | 'bill' | 'assented'` per entry
- **HR-3** — user-visible calc errors unacceptable; UNCOMPUTED gate per measure until Royal Assent (silent admin-side via Phase 41i.6)
- **§9 versioning protocol** — modules ship with skeletons in Stage 1 returning `uncomputedReasons` flags until exposure draft + Royal Assent confirmed; `commencementVerified` flag per measure in `taxYearConfig.ts` (exact pattern from 41e.3 Div 296)

## 🎯 Demo-Complete Critical Path — Lighthouse Adviser Pitch

> **Reza directive 2026-05-04:** the lighthouse adviser pitch waits for a *fully functional* demo of the complete capabilities — not a half-built skeleton, not a demo-mode skin, not a marketing video. "Demo-complete" means the real product, fully working, populated with realistic seeded users — only PROD-hardening (pen test, insurance, CMEK, Cloud Armor, evidence pack, Stripe live mode, training programs, DOCX templates) defers to PROD-ready. Every other Up Next item is bucketed below.
>
> Sequencing rationale: foundation (entity layer) before market layer; entity tree before Sankey (Sankey depends on entity ownership being real); Practice surface before marketplace (advisers need somewhere to receive marketplace requests); Help Center static site runs in parallel from day 1 (different surface, no merge conflict); pitch fixture seed last.
>
> Calendar estimate: **~19 weeks** of focused engineering with sensible parallelism (updated 2026-05-04 per Reza decision D-1: full Phase 41e regulatory engine ships in demo cut, expanding 41e from ~12 days trimmed to ~42 days full). Single engineer ~28 weeks. Bucket annotations on each Up Next entry below.

| Wk | Demo-complete deliverable | Up Next # | Why it's needed for the pitch |
|---|---|---|---|

> **Timeline updated 2026-05-04 per Reza decision D-1.** Original calendar estimate was ~14 weeks of focused engineering (with 41e trimmed to demo-headline cases). Reza directive *"I want all in demo"* expands 41e to all 16 sub-PRs (~42 days). New calendar estimate: **~19 weeks** of focused engineering with sensible parallelism. Rationale: pitching AU advisers without s100A or Div 7A reads as half-built; the +5 weeks buys credibility that a faster but thinner demo cannot.

**🛡️ Explicitly DEFERRED to PROD-ready (post-pitch hardening):**
- Pen test commission + cyber insurance binding (CDR / Basiq accreditation requirement)
- CMEK encryption for Cloud SQL
- Cloud Armor + Security Command Center + KMS enablement
- Compliance evidence pack (screenshots / videos for Basiq submission)
- ~~Phase 41e deep tax cases~~ — **MOVED to demo cut per Reza decision D-1, 2026-05-04. All Phase 41e rules ship pre-pitch.**
- Phase 41f Xero bidirectional sync, transaction-level data, multi-org support
- Phase 32C PR5 (Monitrax `/admin/orgs/*` surface) — Reza works in DB until ~5 paying orgs
- Phase 32C PR6 Stripe LIVE mode + dunning + failed-payment grace + invoice PDFs
- Phase 33c ZIP bundle export (not just single-article PDF)
- Phase 33e training programs (Org Onboarding 5-day, Adviser Certification 8 modules, Compliance Officer Briefing) — lighthouse advisers get white-glove onboarding from Reza directly
- Phase 33f DOCX letterhead templates
- Email-in attack surface hardening at production grade (DKIM/SPF strict mode, Cloud DLP attachment scanning, rate limiting per conversation)
- Conversation transcript 7yr archive infrastructure
- Real ASIC + TPB API automated cross-check (manual at v1)
- Performance hardening (load tests, query optimisation, real-snapshot-feed alert engine vs fixture data)
- Plan-tier polish (rough edges acceptable for pitch; production-clean defers)

| 0G | **Phase 12 Track G — Unified Conversational Onboarding** — DESIGN DOC SHIPPED 2026-05-20 (`docs/blueprint/PHASE_12_TRACK_G_UNIFIED_ONBOARDING.md`). Reza, 2026-05-20: the form wizard and the standalone AI chat are two implementations of one job (§12.1/§12.3 violation); the chat "is clunky, doesn't get the questions, and breaks" (free-form NLP doing structured capture), the form is "dry, old-school". **Decision:** merge into ONE surface — the form wizard stays the system of record for capture; an AI **companion** lives alongside it, narrates each step, reads what the user entered, and reacts with warm, advice-free reflection (guide, not adviser — AFSL boundary). **Guide-only for v1**: extraction retired from the critical path; the "type a sentence" accelerator deferred to G.4. Architecture: KEEP the 12-step wizard spine + Track F `*Sync.ts` layers untouched; EVOLVE `AIHelper`; ADD `CompanionPanel` + `companionGateway` + `/api/onboarding/companion`; DELETE (G.2) the chat script state-machines + `ConversationalSetup` + mode-selector + `CONVERSATIONAL_ONBOARDING` flag. Build sequence: **G.0** companion prototype (household step only — for Reza to evaluate before the full build) → **G.1** companion on all 12 steps → **G.2** retire the standalone chat → **G.3** (= the old F.9) retire `bulk-create` + draft blob → **G.4** optional accelerator → **G.5** (= the old F.11) document upload. **G.0 ✅ DONE — PR #845 merged 2026-05-20** (`CompanionPanel` + `companionGateway` + `/api/onboarding/companion`, static companion on the household step) **+ auth hotfix PR #846** (the companion fetch shipped without the Bearer token → 401 → spurious logout; same class as PR #798 / Tech Debt #20). **Scope sharpened 2026-05-21 (Reza):** the onboarding companion **guides** (leads each step + reacts + bridges to the next) — it is **push-only**, it does NOT field free-form questions and does NOT do extraction. The *full* conversational AI (talk-with-it Q&A) belongs to **My Guide**, a separate surface, **out of Track G scope**. An interim proposal to add a Q&A composer to the onboarding companion was considered + rejected. **G.1 = the conversational guide**: each step becomes a beat — scripted **invitation** + LLM **reaction** + scripted **bridge** (+ G.1b: cross-step **memory** from `WizardData` + **adaptive narration**). **G.1a ✅ DONE — PR #847 merged 2026-05-21** (household step rebuilt in the guided-conversation style). **G.1a pacing iteration ✅ DONE — PR #848 merged 2026-05-21** (paced one-line-at-a-time companion; phase machine resets to greeting on mount). **G.1a accent iteration ✅ DONE — PR #849 merged 2026-05-21** (accent glow halo + typewriter; companion now reads as THE AI surface). **G.2 ✅ DONE 2026-05-21 — standalone chat retired** — branch `claude/track-g2-retire-chat-NL4XV`: Reza — the chat/form toggle + "switch to chat" are redundant now the unified companion exists. Deleted 24 files — the 8 chat script state-machines, `ConversationalSetup` + the `wizard-chat/` UI, `OnboardingModeSelector`, `ConversationalModeToggle`, the `CONVERSATIONAL_ONBOARDING` flag (gate lib + context + public API route + seed entry + admin special-case). `/onboarding` is now always the form wizard + companion — no mode choice. The extraction gateway/tools/schemas + the `chat/extract` route are kept **dormant** for G.4 (per Track G §4). **G.1b ✅ DONE 2026-05-21 — companion on all steps** — branch `claude/track-g1b-companion-all-steps-NL4XV`: the companion rolled from household-only to all **9 entity-collection steps** (household, entities, properties, debts, accounts, investments, super, assets, income-expenses) via a per-step `STEP_CONFIG` (invitation + counts-snapshot + you-summary); `companionGateway` `CompanionStep` widened to 9 + per-step `STEP_BRIEF`; the route validates the step. `welcome` + `review` deliberately have no companion. Legacy `AIHelper.tsx` (mocked-response Q&A drawer) **deleted** — the companion replaces it. Cross-step memory + adaptive narration deferred to a later polish pass. **Legacy-cleanup (Reza asked) ✅ DONE 2026-05-21** — branch `claude/track-g-retire-estimate-service-NL4XV`: deleted the disabled `lib/services/onboardingEstimateService.ts` (threw `OnboardingDisabledError`) + the 6 orphaned `/api/onboarding/estimates/*` routes (goal, housing, household, expenses, income, snapshot) — genuinely dead onboarding code with no live caller (only a stale comment referenced it). 7 files removed. Sequence: **G.0** ✅ → **G.1a** ✅ (#847/#848/#849) → **G.2** ✅ → **G.1b** ✅ → **G.3** (= old F.9) retire `bulk-create` → **G.4** optional accelerator → **G.5** (= old F.11) document upload. **G.3 RESEARCHED + SPEC'D 2026-05-21** (Track G doc §10) — `bulk-create` is NOT a no-op: it still does 6 real things (creates `LegalEntity` rows via a two-pass parent-FK write, writes onboarding completion, INVESTMENT-type income, the CAR→asset link, the BASIQ/IMPORT offset link, the `UserPreference`/draft-blob wipe). So G.3 is a **3-PR effort**: **G.3a** migrate the Entities domain (`entitiesSync.ts` — the last + most complex domain migration: self-referential trust→trustee parent FK two-pass + encrypted-TFN read/write asymmetry + a route gap — `POST /api/entities` doesn't accept `trustType`/`isForeignResident`, must be plumbed); **G.3b** relocate the 4 stragglers off `bulk-create`; **G.3c** delete `bulk-create` + drop the draft blob (Prisma schema migration, §12.12; touches the completion flow). Full executable spec in `PHASE_12_TRACK_G_UNIFIED_ONBOARDING.md` §10. **G.3a ✅ DONE 2026-05-21** — branch `claude/track-g3a-entities-sync-NL4XV`: the Entities domain (the last + most complex) migrated to two-way sync — `lib/onboarding/entitiesSync.ts` (read/diff/sync + mappers, two-pass trust→trustee parent linking) + 14 idempotency tests; entity `POST` route + `createEntity()` service plumbed for `trustType`/`isForeignResident`; `EntitiesStep` migrated (reads on open, registers a commit); `bulk-create` §41b → no-op. No schema migration (entity routes already audit via generic `logCRUD`). The encrypted-TFN read/write asymmetry was resolved by comparing the raw `tfn` value (both sides `''` after a read → idempotent). **G.3b ✅ DONE 2026-05-21** — branch `claude/track-g3b-relocate-stragglers-NL4XV`: the 4 `bulk-create` stragglers (completion write incl. `onboardingProfileType`; BASIQ/IMPORT offset→loan link; CAR→vehicle-asset link; INVESTMENT-type income) relocated into `/api/onboarding/complete` — the end-of-wizard finaliser. `completeOnboarding(wizardData)` now passes the wizard data; the completion write is the critical path, the cross-domain wiring is best-effort (a cosmetic link failure never blocks completion — strictly safer than `bulk-create`'s old all-or-nothing transaction). `bulk-create` §1/§3/§5a/§6 → no-ops. **G.3c ✅ DONE 2026-05-21** — branch `claude/track-g3c-retire-bulkcreate-NL4XV`: `/api/onboarding/bulk-create` **deleted**; its two callers (`page.tsx` `handleComplete`, `DashboardLayout` `handleWizardComplete`) now call only `completeOnboarding(wizardData)` + `clearDraft()`; §8's `country`/`taxYear`/`hasSeenGuidedTour` writes relocated into the `complete` route. **No schema migration** — the `UserPreference.onboardingDraft` column is still load-bearing (autosave + resume seeding + the resume banner); the spec's anticipated column-drop would break resume. Trimming the redundant *entity arrays* out of the draft blob is a future optional `saveDraft` code optimization, deliberately out of scope. **✅ Track G structurally COMPLETE** — onboarding is one unified companion-guided wizard; every domain writes to its real tables; `/api/onboarding/complete` is the sole finaliser. **G.4 ✅ DONE 2026-05-21** — branch `claude/track-g4-describe-accelerator-NL4XV`: the optional "describe it in your own words" accelerator. `lib/onboarding/applyWizardDelta.ts` (pure delta→`WizardData` merge — APPEND-only, 9 tests) + `DescribeItAccelerator.tsx` (collapsed-by-default affordance on the 8 extraction-topic steps) → the dormant `/api/onboarding/chat/extract` engine. The form stays the system of record; the accelerator only appends pre-filled rows, never replaces/deletes — a bad extraction is non-destructive. **G.5 ✅ DONE 2026-05-21** — branch `claude/track-g5-document-upload-NL4XV`: the optional document-upload accelerator (the last Track G item; was F.11). Research found the document→form-fields engine **already exists** — Phase 26.6's `/api/documents/analyze-for-form` (OCR via Vision API / unpdf, AI field mapping via Gemini). So G.5 is "wire it in", not "build a pipeline": `lib/onboarding/mapDocumentFields.ts` (pure mapper — `fieldMappings` → one `IncomeInput`/`ExpenseInput` row, enum-validated, 12 tests) + `DocumentUploadAccelerator.tsx` (collapsed-by-default dropzone on the `income-expenses` step — payslip→income, bill/receipt→expense). **No new endpoint** (SSOT — reuses the canonical engine), no schema change. APPEND-only like G.4 — the form stays the system of record. Loan/property document mapping noted as a future extension. **Companion-guidance beat ✅ DONE 2026-05-21** — branch `claude/companion-guidance-beat-NL4XV`: Reza tested the live wizard — on the Entities step (which always arrives with one auto-created `PERSONAL_NAME` entity) the companion fast-forwarded to *"Nicely done, hit Continue"* within ~10s of landing, congratulating the user for data they never entered. Two fixes in `CompanionPanel.tsx` (one file): (1) **genuine-progress gating** — the panel captures the step's counts signature at mount; the Reaction + Bridge now fire only on progress made *this visit*, so a pre-existing default / resumed-step data no longer triggers a false celebration; (2) a new **checklist beat** between invitation and reaction — each step is now invitation (warm orientation) → checklist (directive completeness prompt — *"add X, Y; don't forget the things people forget"*), and the checklist is the **resting state** the companion stays on, guiding, until the user genuinely engages. No server change, no schema change. **Companion on the welcome step ✅ DONE 2026-05-21** — branch `claude/companion-welcome-step-NL4XV`: Reza tested the live wizard — *"Now I cant see the ai box"* on the first screen. The Track G refactor removed the legacy `AIHelper` and only put the companion on the 9 entity steps, so the `welcome` step (the very first screen) had no AI presence and the greeting did not appear until step 2. Fix: `welcome` added to `STEP_CONFIG` (`CompanionPanel.tsx`) + to `CompanionStep`/`COMPANION_STEPS`/`STEP_BRIEF` (`companionGateway.ts`); the greeting now opens on `welcome`. The companion now hosts all 10 collection steps; only `review` has none. | ~~6b~~ | ✅ **SHIPPED 2026-05-20** — retry-once-on-TLS-error wrapper around the Prisma proxy in `lib/db.ts` with cached-client invalidation. Driven by Reza observing intermittent TLS-42 errors on `/api/health` throughout the day (not just at the 02:00 AEST maintenance-window collision — that was already mitigated 2026-05-19). The trigger condition "if recurrences → ship immediately" fired. Implementation: new `callWithTlsRetry()` helper wraps every Prisma method call through the lazy connector proxy; on TLS handshake error it (a) invalidates `globalForPrisma.prisma` + `prismaInitPromise`, (b) best-effort disconnects the stale pool, (c) retries the invoke once — which re-runs `buildConnectorPrisma()` end-to-end (fresh OIDC → fresh STS → fresh SA impersonation → fresh ephemeral cert). Single-shot only — if the retry also fails, the wrapped error surfaces with the documented Cause #1-#4 list. Safe to retry: TLS handshake fails BEFORE any SQL is sent, so no partial-write risk on writes. Updated `04_WIF_TROUBLESHOOTING.md §3.G` with new Cause #5 + flipped "Long-term hardening (queued)" → "✅ SHIPPED 2026-05-20". | | |

| ~~7~~ | ✅ **PR 3c.2c balance-write audit SHIPPED 2026-05-18 (PR I, this PR)** — closes §6A.1 item #6 (app-wide `balanceLastUpdatedAt` write-site audit). Audited every `prisma.account.{create, update, upsert, updateMany}` site in the codebase (27 hits across 16 files). Found **3 active gaps** where `currentBalance` was written but `balanceSource` + `balanceLastUpdatedAt` were not — chip + nudge from PR F would silently lie on accounts touched by these paths: (a) `POST /api/accounts` (manual create — was creating accounts that appeared as default MANUAL with **no timestamp**, immediately stale), (b) `PATCH /api/accounts/[id]` (user balance edit — never refreshed `balanceLastUpdatedAt` so a freshly-edited manual balance appeared as old as its row's creation date), (c) `POST /api/basiq/sync` route's local `syncAccount()` helper (Basiq-synced accounts appeared as default MANUAL — the chip lied "Manual · 4mo ago" on accounts the sync touched yesterday). Plus 1 placeholder-create site (`POST /api/accounts/[id]/import`'s `currentBalance: 0` placeholder) tagged as IMPORT for chip honesty. New SSOT helper `lib/utils/accountBalance.ts` → `balanceWriteFields(source)` returns `{ balanceSource, balanceLastUpdatedAt: new Date() }` for spread into Prisma `data`; all 4 sites now spread it. The 7 already-correct sites (`bank/import`, `accounts/[id]/balance`, `bank/basiqSync.ts` worker path, `unified-transactions/cash`, `onboarding/bulk-create`, `cashAccount.ts` create branch, `cashAccount.ts` update branch is a no-op for balance) left unchanged — they were inline-correct and consistency-refactoring them adds churn without behaviour change. The `PATCH` fix is conditional (`balanceChanging = currentBalance !== undefined`) so a rename / re-type / rate change does NOT falsely refresh the timestamp — that would have flipped a stale-MANUAL chip green for the wrong reason. Doc-sync: `PHASE_12_WIZARD_REDESIGN_PLAN.md` §6A.1 item #6 marked complete in the row above. No schema change. No CDR posture change. Reviewer-reject rule for future PRs: every new `prisma.account.{create, update, upsert}` that writes `currentBalance` MUST spread `...balanceWriteFields(source)`. | | |

Dictionary `lib/help/tooltips.ts` — SSOT per CLAUDE.md §12.2; 30 entries: 22 finance terms (`lvr`, `equity`, `rental-yield`, `effective-principal`, `offset-account`, `concessional-cap`, `non-concessional-cap`, `superannuation-guarantee`, `division-293`, `division-296`, `transfer-balance-cap`, `tbar`, `sole-purpose-test`, `in-house-asset-cap`, `lrba`, `cgt-discount`, `franking-credit`, `division-7a-loan`, `medicare-levy`, `lito-sapto`, `ppr`, `negative-gearing`) — each with ATO/ASIC/SIS Act source-cite — plus 8 Monitrax-specific (`entity-role`, `entity-type`, `trail-stage`, `health-score`, `ownership-scope`, `audience`, `surface-tag`, `consent-status`). Wire-ups in this PR: `Equity` + `LVR` on `PropertyTile`; `Concessional` + `Non-Concessional` + `Medicare Levy` on `app/dashboard/tax/page.tsx`; `Effective Balance` on `LoanDetailDialog`; `Tag` on adviser feedback form. Full inline rollout to remaining label sites is incremental thereafter (no engineering blocker — just edits). | Phase 33 | SHIPPED |
| ~~39~~ | ✅ **Phase 33g.2 SHIPPED 2026-05-10 (this PR).** Reza directive: ship the consumer surface even if budget caps aren't set yet — UI is identical either way; AI behaviour gated on `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` env presence. New floating `<FeedbackButton />` on consumer dashboard chrome (top-right, slightly LEFT of `<HelpDrawerButton />`, suppressed during onboarding). Opens `<FeedbackChatDrawer />` — same warm-ivory glass shape as Phase 33b help drawer; right-edge slide-in ≥md / bottom-sheet 85vh <md. **Two server-decided modes** via new `/api/portal/feedback/ai-status` endpoint: (a) **AI on** — first message creates thread, AI Haiku 4.5 reply auto-triggered fire-and-forget on the server, polled by client for ≤12s, max 6 AI turns per thread, hard guards on already-Reza-handled status; (b) **AI off** (key absent) — same UI, no AI typing indicator, success message points the user at the 48hr SLA. Schema extension: `FeedbackAuthorRole` gains `CONSUMER` + `CLAUDE_AI` values (additive migration `prisma/migrations/20260510120000_add_feedback_ai_role`); `AuditAction` gains `FEEDBACK_AI_REPLIED` (separate from the human-reply audit so cost analytics can attribute AI tokens). New `lib/ai/anthropic.ts` client + `respondToFeedbackThread()` in `feedbackService.ts` — system prompt enforces AFSL boundary (the AI is structurally incapable of giving financial product advice; redirects financial questions to `/dashboard/cfo` AI Guide). `createThread` + `appendReply` extended to accept `CONSUMER` author role; ownership check broadened from "ADVISER === own threads" to "any human === own threads". New env var `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` (registered in `docs/operational/cost-control/00_VENDOR_INVENTORY.md` Tier 2 + `01_BUDGET_ALERTS_SETUP.md` §4). CDR posture: AI ONLY sees conversation text; never user snapshot or CDR data. Audit posture: every AI reply writes `FEEDBACK_AI_REPLIED` with `{model, tokensIn, tokensOut, turnIndex}` for spend attribution. Errors caught + audit-logged with status=FAILURE; user-facing reply path NEVER breaks on AI errors. Anthropic SDK added (`@anthropic-ai/sdk@^0.30.1`). | Phase 33 | SHIPPED |

(a) D2C user with no professional linked → button opens an invite-only marketplace picker (Monitrax-curated 10–20 launch professionals; verified ASIC AFSL / ACL / TPB before listing). (b) Org-attached user → button pre-routes to the org's roster (grouped by discipline — Sarah for wealth, Tom for tax, Jess for refinance — all inside the org's branded experience). (c) Org-attached users see ONLY their org's professionals (Reza decision: org pays for Monitrax to be their CRM + comms channel; can't be a leaky funnel to competitors). The "next-level" advice is always human; the AI is structurally incapable of crossing the AFSL line. Two new revenue streams open up: (1) D2C marketplace lead fee tiered by user profile (AU$80 sub-AU$500k / AU$150 AU$500k–$2M / AU$250 AU$2M+), (2) Org subscription bundles all professional seats AND all client Wealth-tier features (no per-client charge), so the org's incentive to push the platform to their entire client book aligns naturally. **Closes Q-PRA-1.** Reversal logged below. ## ↩️ Reversed Decisions

> Things we tried and rolled back. **Do not re-attempt without explicit user confirmation.** Each entry: what was tried, why reverted, lesson.

| Date | What was tried | Why reverted | Lesson |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-04-30 | Append `?connection_limit=1&pool_timeout=20` to `DATABASE_URL` env var on Vercel | Caused 100% of API requests to 401. Prisma's URL parser broke on the combination of unencoded `@`/`%` in the password + new query params. | Don't append to a fragile URL. Either rotate password to URL-safe chars first, OR apply `connection_limit` via Prisma datasource override (Option α). Tracked in Up Next #7. |
| 2026-04-29 | Add `calculateOffsetInterestSavings(offsetBalance, loanAnnualRate)` to `lib/utils/calculations.ts` | User direction: "the calculations and relationships on all the legacy and current pages are correct, so don't create new logics without confirmation … use the existing logics and engines for the new changes." | No new calc engines without explicit go-ahead. Compose existing primitives (`calculateEffectivePrincipal`, `calculateInterestForPeriod`) only when behaviour preservation is verified. |
| 2026-04-29 | Compose offset interest savings as `interestForPeriod(principal, rate, 1) − interestForPeriod(effective, rate, 1)` (would correctly cap savings when `offset >= principal`) | Even though "more correct" in the edge case, behaviour deviates from the legacy `offsetBalance × loanAnnualRate`. User direction: existing math is correct. | Mathematical "correctness" is not the bar. **Preserved behaviour** is. New calc behaviour requires explicit user sign-off. |
| 2026-04-15 | `prisma db push` in Vercel build script | R12 incident — silently dropped legacy tables on every deploy. Lost data risk. | Build scripts NEVER run `db push`. Only `prisma migrate deploy`. Schema changes need a migration file (CLAUDE.md §12.12). |
| 2026-05-04 | Two-voice AI model for D2C vs org-attached users (`voiceContext: 'self' \| 'professional-attached'` parameter on AI advisor) | Reza counter-proposal: single-voice AI for everyone + "Ask a Professional" affordance is a cleaner architecture, simpler AFSL boundary, AND opens a second revenue surface (marketplace lead fees). Voice-switching adapter would have added test surface and drift risk for no compensating benefit. | (a) Architecturally simpler is almost always better — `voiceContext` was a clever solution to a problem we didn't need to have. (b) AFSL boundaries are best enforced *structurally* (the AI is incapable of crossing them) rather than *editorially* (a per-context output adapter). (c) Always look for the answer that opens new revenue rather than the answer that closes a legal gap; the marketplace + ask-a-pro model does both. |
| 2026-05-08 | **Hamburger-drawer primary navigation on phones** (sidebar slide-in triggered from a `Menu` icon in the mobile header; sub-tabs only reachable by re-opening the drawer). | Two-tap-to-section + two-tap-to-sub-tab = four-tap path to a known destination. Hidden navigation makes the TRAIL journey invisible at the moment users most need orientation (financial-stress UX research, Mani et al. 2013). Apple/Robinhood/Cash App/Up Bank all use a persistent bottom tab bar on phones; hamburger is desktop-secondary nav, not mobile-primary nav. iPad portrait <1024px also got the phone drawer, missing the tablet rail tier entirely. **Replaced 2026-05-08 by Phase 14.6** — 5-tab bottom nav mapped to TRAIL stages + horizontal sub-tab pill row + dedicated tablet sidebar at the `md:` (768px) breakpoint. | (a) On phones, primary nav must be visible at all times — the IA *is* the behavioural intervention. (b) Tablet is a first-class tier, not a "narrow desktop"; it gets the persistent rail. (c) When IA matches a published framework (TRAIL), make the framework the visible IA — don't hide it behind a hamburger. (d) **Never re-introduce hamburger primary nav on phones** without explicit user sign-off; if a future surface needs more than 5 destinations, add to MoreSheet, not to the bottom bar. |
| 2026-05-11 | **Consumer-first GTM** (SEO content engine + paid ads + creator partnerships + freemium acquisition funnel as the *primary* go-to-market motion for the next 90 days) | Considered and rejected during the 2026-05-11 architect-mode GTM synthesis. Three structural reasons: (a) **Basiq economics** — $10k upfront + $2k/mo + ~1mo onboarding means we need ≥AU$3–5k MRR funding it *before* paying it. Consumer subscriptions take 12–18 months to reach that MRR from a cold start; B2B can reach it in 90 days from one signed broker contract. (b) **Pre-Basiq product gap** — the killer feature ("connect your banks, see everything") is unavailable until Basiq is live. Selling consumers a manual-entry PFM when Frollo/WeMoney/PocketSmith/Up offer auto bank feeds for free is a losing pitch. (c) **CAC payback** — AU consumer fintech CAC payback is brutal (12+ months); we have no runway for that even if the unit economics worked. **Replaced 2026-05-11 by B2B-led GTM** — `docs/marketing/GTM_EXECUTION_PLAN.md` Phases 1–4 (broker pilots + Review service) front-load 90-day revenue + warm subscription leads; consumer scale becomes Phase 6, post-Basiq. | (a) Don't fight Basiq economics — sequence the GTM to fund the gate, don't pay the gate to enable an unvalidated motion. (b) Pre-accreditation, the product's perceived value to a consumer is a fraction of what it'll be post-Basiq — sell to buyers who don't need the auto-feed (brokers using their own loan data) first. (c) **Do not re-attempt consumer-first GTM as the primary 90-day motion** without explicit user sign-off. Consumer waitlist + a single high-leverage creator partnership can run in parallel as low-cost preparation, but the burn-rate engine (SEO content production, paid ads, influencer fees) does not turn on until Phase 6 trigger fires. |
| 2026-05-07 | **Pending-actions popup-on-login** (modal-on-arrival overlay on `/dashboard` that bundled up to 3 actions; PR6.5b initial implementation) | Behavioural-friction risk: years of cookie banners + newsletter modals + permission prompts have wired users to X-out *anything* that blocks the page on arrival, regardless of content. Even with snooze + opt-out, the first impression of every day became "things you owe Monitrax" rather than "look how your wealth is going" — direct opposite of the warm-words / no-shame framing in CLAUDE.md §14. Fogg Behavior Model: anxiety reduces ability — a daily anxiety pulse makes the categorisation task *harder*, not easier. Apple/Linear/Stripe almost never interrupt with "things to do" modals on landing; modals-on-login are a SaaS re-engagement pattern, not a premium-product pattern. Reza decision: *"go with your recommendations"* → pivoted to **non-modal collapsible strip** (Option A) within the same PR. Same SSOT aggregator + API + once-per-day gate; presentation-layer swap only. | (a) Pending-actions FRAMING is sound (the *what*); modal DELIVERY was wrong (the *how*). (b) Non-blocking surfaces > blocking surfaces for routine engagement — the user finds them when they're ready instead of being interrupted. (c) When a pattern is structurally indistinguishable from a nag (cookie banner / newsletter modal / permission prompt), users dismiss reflexively before reading — the message never lands. (d) **Never re-introduce a modal-on-login pattern for routine engagement on `/dashboard` without explicit user sign-off**, even if the data behind it is genuinely useful. Strips, badges, in-context affordances — those are the right surfaces. |

- **Tech Debt #18 — Prod schema-drift CDR tables corrective migration SHIPPED (Day 2 session 5).** **Closes Tech Debt #18 + Phase 0 console row #11.** Reza ran the audit (after PR #806 fixed the endpoint's deserialization bug); JSON showed 3 missing tables + 4 missing enums in prod — all CDR-critical Phase L Fix G18/G43 work that was applied via the historical `prisma db push` workflow and never got a migration file (same root cause as the 2026-05-12 `basiq_connections` drift). New migration `20260519100000_fix_pre_migration_cdr_tables_drift/migration.sql`: 4 `CREATE TYPE` blocks wrapped in `DO $$ ... EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL ... END $$` (Postgres idempotency pattern) + 3 `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` + 3 FK `ALTER TABLE ADD CONSTRAINT` in DO blocks + 7 `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS`. Purely additive — §12.11 N/A by structural argument. On dev the migration is a complete no-op (every object already exists via `db push`); on prod the missing objects get created in the same `prisma migrate deploy` pass the next deploy runs. Existing consumers (`app/api/admin/cdr/{compliance,complaints,consent}/route.ts`) already have `.catch(() => 0)` defensive wrapping so they degraded gracefully today; once this deploys they'll return real counts. **Pre-Basiq engineering blocker resolved.** No further audit needed unless a future PR adds tables via `db push`.

- **fix(admin): schema-drift endpoint deserialization bug (Day 2 session 4).** Reza tried to run the audit (`GET /api/admin/schema-drift`) — got HTTP 500 with `QUERY_FAILED` + Prisma error "Failed to deserialize column of type 'name'". Root cause: 3 raw SQL queries in `app/api/admin/schema-drift/route.ts` select columns of Postgres's internal `name` type (`information_schema.tables.table_name`, `information_schema.columns.{table_name, column_name}`, `pg_type.typname`, `pg_enum.enumlabel`, `pg_namespace.nspname`) — Prisma's `$queryRawUnsafe` driver can't deserialize this type. Fix: cast each column to `::text AS <alias>` in the SELECT clauses. Three queries fixed. Endpoint was added in PR #743's tooling sweep + never exercised against prod until today. Doc-sync: this Recently Completed entry + `CHANGELOG_2026_05_19.md` Session 4. After deploy, Reza re-runs the audit to get the actual drift JSON.

The 2026-04-10 tech debt was filed when several detail dialogs hadn't been built; Phase 37 + Phase 38 closed the gap. **#6** (`DIRECT_URL` env var) — codebase audit `grep -rn 'DIRECT_URL'` returns ZERO references; the only hit is the tech debt row itself. - **Phase 12 PR 3c.2c — Balance-write audit + canonical helper SHIPPED (PR I, session 24).** Closes `PHASE_12_WIZARD_REDESIGN_PLAN.md` §6A.1 item #6 (app-wide `balanceLastUpdatedAt` write-site audit). The chip + nudge from PR F + the upgrade button from PR H are only honest if every code path that writes `Account.currentBalance` also writes the source + timestamp. This PR is the defensive sweep that makes that invariant a structural property, not a discipline question. **Audit method:** grep every `prisma.account.{create, update, upsert, updateMany}` call in active code (27 hits across 16 files); classify each as "touches currentBalance" vs "doesn't"; for the touchers, verify both `balanceSource` + `balanceLastUpdatedAt` are also written. **Found 3 active gaps + 1 placeholder gap:** (a) `POST /api/accounts` manual create — was creating accounts with no source tag + no timestamp → chip would say "Manual" with no age suffix (the staleness branch never fired). (b) `PATCH /api/accounts/[id]` — when user edits balance, did NOT refresh `balanceLastUpdatedAt` → a freshly-edited manual balance appeared as old as its row's creation date; the staleness nudge would fire on accounts the user JUST updated. (c) `POST /api/basiq/sync` route's local `syncAccount()` helper updates `currentBalance` but didn't tag `balanceSource: 'BASIQ'` → Basiq-synced accounts appeared as default MANUAL in the chip (the chip lied "Manual · 4mo ago" on accounts the sync touched yesterday). (d) `POST /api/accounts/[id]/import` placeholder-create (`currentBalance: 0` before import enrichment) — tagged as IMPORT for chip honesty from the moment the row exists. **Fix:** new SSOT helper `lib/utils/accountBalance.ts` exports `balanceWriteFields(source: BalanceSource, now?: Date): { balanceSource, balanceLastUpdatedAt }`. Caller spreads into Prisma `data`: `data: { currentBalance: 12345, ...balanceWriteFields('USER_VERIFIED') }`. All 4 sites converted. The `PATCH` fix is conditional (`balanceChanging = currentBalance !== undefined`) so a rename / re-type / rate change does NOT falsely refresh the timestamp — that would have flipped a stale-MANUAL chip green for the wrong reason. **7 already-correct sites** left unchanged (`bank/import` × 2, `accounts/[id]/balance`, `bank/basiqSync.ts` worker × 2, `unified-transactions/cash`, `onboarding/bulk-create`, `cashAccount.ts` create) — refactoring them to use the helper is consistency churn without behaviour change; flagged as optional follow-up. **Out of scope** (audited but skipped): `prisma/seed-*.ts` (test fixtures, not load-bearing); `lib/testing/loader.ts` (test loader); `lib/db/tenant.ts` (multi-tenant scaffold not yet active). **Reviewer-reject rule** for future PRs: every new `prisma.account.{create, update, upsert}` that writes `currentBalance` MUST spread `...balanceWriteFields(source)`. The helper file's JSDoc states this in plain English; reviewers should cite it in code review. No schema change. No CDR posture change.

- **Phase 12 PR 3c.2a — "Upgrade this account" button SHIPPED (PR H, session 23).** Closes `PHASE_12_WIZARD_REDESIGN_PLAN.md` §6A.1 item #4 — the visibility→action loop kicked off by PR F's chip + nudge. New shared primitive `components/accounts/UpgradeAccountButton.tsx` (~80 LOC) — renders nothing for accounts whose `balanceSource ∈ {BASIQ, IMPORT}` (already on a fresher tier — the chip already tells the user how recent their balance is). For `balanceSource ∈ {MANUAL, USER_VERIFIED}` it renders two CTAs: "Connect via Basiq" (emerald, gated on `useBasiqEnabled()` — hides cleanly when the feature flag is off) + "Upload statement" (slate outline, always visible). Both are `<Link>` deep-links to the existing `/dashboard/balances?action=connect-basiq` and `?action=import` handlers (Phase 36 PR 2b/2c) — **no parallel flows**, no new endpoints. An optional `onBeforeNavigate` prop lets callers (the dialog) close themselves before the navigation fires, so the page-level action handler doesn't run while the dialog is still on screen. `AccountDetail` type (`components/accounts/AccountDetailDialog.tsx`) extended with `balanceSource?: string | null` + `balanceLastUpdatedAt?: string | null` — both columns already exist on the Prisma row + already pass through to the page state, so the cast `account as AccountDetail` is now type-honest. The dialog Overview tab's Account Details card gets a new "Data source" row (rendering the canonical `<DataSourceChip>`) + a divider + a calm one-line label ("Keep this balance fresh — connect a feed or upload a statement.") + the upgrade button — only when `balanceSource ∈ {MANUAL, USER_VERIFIED}`. CLAUDE.md §0 lens reads: financial-adviser → neutral framing (no shaming of manual entry; emerald accent reserved for the freshest tier); behaviour-psychologist → soft suggestion, not a forcing function (users with cash / crypto / foreign accounts who legitimately need MANUAL can ignore it); architect → §12.2 SSOT — one upgrade primitive, one set of deep-link targets; designer → restraint, a small row inside the existing Account Details card, not a banner or modal. No schema change. No API change. No CDR posture change. Doc-sync: `PHASE_12_WIZARD_REDESIGN_PLAN.md` §6A not flipped — the doc treats §6A.1 as one chunk; flip happens when all five items ship.

- **Phase 42 PR3.5.1 — Receipt-picker UI SHIPPED (PR G, session 22).** Closes the forward-direction half of Up Next #45 — `<ReceiptCandidatePicker>` modal consumes the existing `PICK_FROM` verdict from `/api/documents/analyze/confirm` (composite score 0.7–0.95 — high enough to be plausible, too ambiguous for AUTO_LINK at ≥0.95). Previously the verdict was returned by the route but **dropped by every UI** (verdict shipped Phase 42 PR3 2026-05-07 with the explicit note: "PICK_FROM → return verdict; UI prompts user. No DB mutation here — the user-picker endpoint will be a follow-up (PR3.5)"). New `lib/bookkeeping/receiptMatcher.ts` exports `linkReceiptToTransaction()` — extracted SSOT helper that wraps the link mutation + audit-log write + ownership check + already-bound check; both the AUTO_LINK branch in `/api/documents/analyze/confirm` AND the new `POST /api/bookkeeping/receipts/pick-match` call it (CLAUDE.md §12.3 — one link path for both branches). New endpoint accepts `{ documentId, expenseId, transactionId }`, returns 404 (`RECEIPT_LINK_NOT_FOUND`) or 409 (`RECEIPT_LINK_ALREADY_BOUND`) with typed `ReceiptLinkError`; uses `withPermission('transaction.write')`. `<ReceiptCandidatePicker>` UI: Apple-quiet modal (rounded card / slate ink / emerald accent for top candidate / hover-lift rows); confidence dot (emerald ≥0.85 / sky 0.75–0.85 / amber 0.7–0.75 — three bands keyed to the matcher thresholds, no % surfaced — psychology lens: percentages invite reverse-engineering); date · merchant · amount per candidate; "Skip — none of these match" first-class option closes without forcing a link (creates orphan receipt, user lives with it); ESC + click-outside + X all close. Wired into `/dashboard/documents` Smart Inbox: `handleConfirmAnalysis` now reads the response `receiptMatch` field after a successful POST and opens the picker when `kind === 'PICK_FROM' && entity.type === 'EXPENSE'`; the picker's `onLinked` fires `setRefreshKey` to re-pull the documents list. Refactor net: `-16 LOC` in the confirm route (dead `pickLinkFields` + `recordTransactionEdit` imports removed; the inline link block collapsed to one shared call). Doc-sync: `PHASE_42_CONSUMER_BOOKKEEPING_COMPLETION.md` not updated — that doc treats PR3.5 as one chunk; PR3.5.2 (reverse-direction matcher) flip happens when PR3.5.2 ships. No schema change. No CDR posture change.

- **Phase 12 PR 3c.1 — Data-source hygiene VISIBILITY SLICE SHIPPED (PR F, session 21).** Closes the visibility half of the data-source-hygiene story spec'd in `PHASE_12_WIZARD_REDESIGN_PLAN.md` §6A.1 items #1 + #2. New shared primitive `components/accounts/DataSourceChip.tsx` — renders a small freshness chip next to any rendered Account balance, mapping `(balanceSource, balanceLastUpdatedAt)` to one of five tones: BASIQ → emerald "Synced 2m ago", IMPORT → sky "Imported 3d ago", USER_VERIFIED → indigo "Verified 1d ago", MANUAL fresh (<14d) → slate "Manual · 4d ago", MANUAL stale (≥14d) → amber "Manual · 32d ago"; renders nothing when source is null. Behaviour-psychologist tone choices: slate (not red) for stale-manual (hygiene nudge, not alarm); emerald reserved for live Open-Banking sync (strongest trust state). Companion primitive `components/dashboard/StaleBalanceNudge.tsx` — top-of-page banner that fires when ≥1 MANUAL account has aged past 14 days; calm copy ("Your dashboard reads from these balances — keeping them fresh keeps every number it shows you accurate") + two CTAs deep-linking to the canonical `/dashboard/balances?action=connect-basiq` and `?action=import` (no parallel routes); session-only dismiss via sessionStorage (reappears next session if still stale — right cadence for a hygiene nudge, not a one-and-done modal). Single SSOT `isBalanceStale()` exported from the chip module + consumed by the nudge — one place to change the threshold. Wired into `/dashboard/balances`: replaced the inline `isBasiq && <Badge>...</Badge>` Basiq-only badge with `<DataSourceChip>` (now covers all 4 sources; CLAUDE.md §12.2 — one canonical chip, not 4 inline cases); mounted `<StaleBalanceNudge>` between the page hero and the Hidden Wealth lens. Dead imports cleaned (`Zap` + `Badge` no longer used on that page). No schema change (the columns already exist on `Account` + the route already returns them — no API change either). No CDR posture change. Items 3–7 of §6A.1 (confidence indicators on derived metrics, "Upgrade this account" button, first-visit migration modal, balance-age heat-map sub-route, app-wide write-site audit) reshelved as **PR 3c.2** in Up Next — they can ship independently, in any order.

- **Phase 42 PR5.5 — PDF summary + receipt ZIP bundle SHIPPED (PR E, session 20).** Two new exporter modules under `lib/bookkeeping/taxPack/`: `pdfExporter.ts` (`buildTaxPackPdf(summary)` — `pdfkit`, A4, 6 sections — cover with FY label + generated date / totals / ATO labels table / per-property P&L blocks with sorted expense breakdowns / data-sources disclosure with per-source counts + month coverage / on-every-page footer disclaimer with horizontal rule) and `zipBundleBuilder.ts` (`buildReceiptZipBundle(userId, window)` — bundles every `Document` in the FY window where `category IN (RECEIPT, INVOICE, TAX)`; foldered by category — `Receipts/` / `Invoices/` / `Tax_Documents/` — with `<yyyy-MM-dd>_<originalFilename>` naming, dedup-counter for collisions, plain-text `MANIFEST.txt` with FY label + window dates + per-folder counts + missing count + disclaimer). **§12.7 dep decision:** added **only** `pdfkit` (one new dep, MIT, ~700KB, well-trodden Node PDF library — no GCP managed alternative exists for JSON-to-PDF generation); **dropped** the originally-planned `archiver` dep — reused the already-installed `jszip` instead, mirroring the canonical pattern at `/api/documents/export/route.ts`. Net: one new prod dep instead of two. The Up Next #47 row called for both — the substituted `jszip` choice is the smaller, cleaner answer. Tax Pack export route (`app/api/bookkeeping/tax-pack/export/route.ts`) extended to accept `format=pdf` and `format=zip`, with proper `Content-Type`/`Content-Disposition` per format + diagnostic response headers (`X-Monitrax-Doc-Count`, `X-Monitrax-Doc-Missing` for ZIP); the `format=zip` branch short-circuits the summary aggregation (zip doesn't need transactions, only Documents). `<TaxPackExportButton />` in `components/bookkeeping/TaxPackExportButton.tsx` gains 2 new format options (PDF set as default — most natural artefact for a user-to-accountant handoff), with filename derivation now switching basename between `monitrax-tax-pack-*` and `monitrax-receipts-*` per format. No schema change. No CDR posture change — receipt bytes are user-uploaded artefacts (§13.3), not CDR data; the manifest exposes only FY + counts. Doc-sync: `docs/blueprint/PHASE_42_CONSUMER_BOOKKEEPING_COMPLETION.md` PR5.5 row flipped from "queued" to ✅ SHIPPED with the dep-decision rationale captured inline; `docs/policy/APPROVED_DEPENDENCIES.md` gains the `pdfkit` row (Utilities table) + the `@types/pdfkit` row (dev deps Type Definitions table) — both dated 2026-05-18.

- **Phase 32B PR3 polish item ③ — richer real-data client-book table SHIPPED (PR D, session 19).** `/portal/clients` list now shows the human-dimension fields alongside the system fields. `app/portal/clients/page.tsx` fetches `GET /api/portal/clients?organizationId=…` in parallel with the granular client list (the same aggregate endpoint the dashboard hero strip reads), builds a `Map<clientId, { trailStage, healthScore, healthDelta, activeAlertCount }>`, and feeds it to `<ClientList>` via the new optional `aggregateMap` prop. `components/portal/clients/ClientList.tsx` gains `ClientAggregateRow` interface + the `aggregateMap?` prop + 3 conditional columns inserted between Consent and Assigned To: **TRAIL** (`TrailStagePill` — 5-stage warm-toned pill: Track=sky, Reduce=amber, Anchor=emerald, Invest=indigo, Live=fuchsia; `—` when stage null), **Health** (`HealthCell` — score colour-toned by band: emerald ≥70 / amber 40–69 / rose <40 — plus a delta indicator `↑ +N` (emerald) / `↓ -N` (rose) / `stable` (neutral grey); `—` when score null), **Alerts** (count pill, rose-toned when >0 else slate). Aggregate fetch failures are **non-blocking** — the granular table still renders, the 3 new columns just collapse out (the existing 5 columns stay). Privacy posture (§13.3): no balances / no CDR data flows through the map — aggregate scalars + display labels only, byte-for-byte the same data the dashboard hero strip already shows. The product question raised in #9b — how much per-client aggregate to surface on the practice landing vs. behind the per-client drill-in (each drill-in writes a `ClientAccessLog` row but landing aggregates don't) — resolved by the architect lens: putting the aggregate on `/portal/clients` (the proper client-book surface) rather than the dashboard landing keeps the landing slim (the slim summary card from #9b stays) and keeps the per-client drill-in audit trail intact. Closes the §6b polish backlog (① + ② + ③ all shipped). No schema change. No new endpoint. No CDR posture change. tsc clean against existing patterns (`Map.get` + nullish-coalescing matches `aggregateMap` consumers elsewhere); Vercel preview is the canonical type check per §11.2.

Fix-forward migration `prisma/migrations/20260514130000_fix_basiq_connection_consent_columns/migration.sql` — `ALTER TABLE "basiq_connections" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS "consentExpiresAt" TIMESTAMP(3)` + `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS "consentScope" TEXT` + `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ...`. Idempotent (`IF NOT EXISTS` → a no-op on dev where the columns already exist via `db push`, adds them on prod). Purely additive — §12.11 N/A. Per-source counts in `summary.dataSources.sources`; per-month BASIQ-vs-imported-vs-manual breakdown so the accountant can assess provenance. **PDF + ZIP receipt bundle deferred to PR5.5** (need `pdfkit` + `archiver` — net-new packages that warrant separate dep-review). The CSV + XLSX + JSON exports already cover the accountant's primary workflow. Build + typecheck + tests clean.

- **Phase 42 PR4 — QIF parity layer SHIPPED (this PR).** Fourth sub-PR of six. Goal: bring QIF / CSV / OFX imports up to ~85% of BASIQ-grade categorisation accuracy. **MCC catalog** — new `lib/bank/mccCatalog.ts` with 47 high-volume AU merchants mapped to ISO 18245 codes (Coles 5411, Bunnings 5200, AGL 4900, Telstra 4814, Netflix 4899, Uber 4121 with Uber Eats 5814 ordered first to win the substring race, etc.). Substring-match-on-normalised-merchant; first-pattern-wins; per CLAUDE.md §12.2 SSOT — `lookupMCC` is the ONE function that resolves "what MCC does this merchant have?" Applied at import time in two places: `lib/bank/normalisation.ts:normaliseTransaction` (canonical pipeline) + `app/api/unified-transactions/route.ts:handleBatchImport` (standalone path). When BASIQ later supersedes a row (Phase 13.10), BASIQ-supplied MCC overrides automatically. **QIF L-field seed** — parser propagates `tx.category` → `RawTransaction.bankSuppliedCategory`; normaliser surfaces it on `NormalisedTransaction.bankSuppliedCategory`; new `mapBankSuppliedCategory()` helper in `lib/bank/categorisation.ts` maps ~15 common bank-side category strings (Salary / Rent income / Council rates / Strata / Insurance / Streaming etc.) to Monitrax canonical labels at confidence 0.75 (= 0.6 base + 0.15 boost per spec §4 PR4) — positioned BELOW user/system rules (0.9, authoritative) but ABOVE the AI fallback. QIF `[Account]` transfer brackets route to Transfer / Internal automatically. **Bank-statement sanity-check** — new `lib/bookkeeping/importSanity.ts:computeBalanceSanityCheck()` pure helper. Compares opening + sum vs stated closing within $0.50 tolerance. Returns structured result the API surfaces in `data.sanity`. New `<ImportSanityBanner />` component renders an amber, dismissable banner ("$30 between imported transactions and the closing balance — likely a transaction we didn't catch."). **Re-import merge preview** — new `dryRun: true` flag on the batch-import API. Returns `computeDedupPreview()` verdict (totalCandidates / duplicateCount / newCount / sample) + the sanity-check WITHOUT writing rows. The Import Wizard can call this before commit to render a "12 of 50 match existing — merge / overwrite / skip?" picker. Per CLAUDE.md §12.3 the dedup helper composes PR1's `computeDescriptionHash` — no parallel hash logic. **No migration in this PR** — all PR4 deliverables are application-layer (TypeScript-only type extensions, no schema changes). **Tests:** 78 new unit tests across `tests/bookkeeping/{mccCatalog,importSanity,qifLFieldSeed}.test.ts` (catalog hygiene, normalisation correctness, all 11 high-volume merchants pinned, Uber-vs-UberEats-disambiguation order, sanity-check boundary at $0.50, dedup-preview account scoping + BASIQ exclusion + sample-cap-at-5, L-field seed routing for 8 categories + rule-engine-wins precedence). **Cumulative: 120/120 green** (PR1 27 + PR2 13 + PR3 32 + PR4 48). **BASIQ-on/BASIQ-off behaviour:** when BASIQ supersedes a row, BASIQ MCC + native category override the catalog seed. Catalog continues to seed any QIF/CSV import the user runs while BASIQ is connected. The L-field seed is parser-level (QIF only) and BASIQ rows ignore it.

- **Phase 42 PR3 — Receipt ↔ transaction matching + cash quick-add + PWA camera capture SHIPPED (this PR).** Third sub-PR of six. **Schema:** new enum value `TransactionSource.RECEIPT` for receipts that did NOT match an existing bank/QIF row; new `AccountType.CASH` for the sole-trader / market-seller cash log (added to `LIQUID_ACCOUNT_TYPES` so net-worth + emergency-fund snapshots see it as liquid); new `unified_transactions.matchedDocumentId` column for direct receipt → transaction lookup (complements the existing many-to-many DocumentLink table). Migration `20260510220000_phase_42_pr3_receipts_cash` — additive only (ALTER TYPE × 2 + ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN × 1 nullable; §12.11 N/A). **Service:** new `lib/bookkeeping/receiptMatcher.ts` — single SSOT for the threshold rules (D-42-7 signed-off): 3-day window, $0.50 abs / 0.5% rel amount tolerance, Levenshtein vendor similarity, 50% / 35% / 15% composite weighting with strong-signal override (same-day + exact-amount → auto-link grade even on vendor jitter). DB-coarse-filter + JS exact-scoring pattern; defends against double-linking by skipping rows with `source = 'RECEIPT'` or `matchedDocumentId IS NOT NULL`. `findReceiptMatches` returns AUTO_LINK / PICK_FROM / NO_MATCH verdict; AUTO_LINK requires the top candidate to beat the 2nd by ≥0.05 to avoid ambiguous links. New `lib/bookkeeping/cashAccount.ts` — idempotent `getOrCreateCashAccount()` with three-case resolution (existing CASH-typed → use; existing "Cash"-named → adopt by re-tagging; otherwise → fresh). **DME wire-up:** `app/api/documents/analyze/confirm/route.ts` extends the CREATE_EXPENSE path with `applyReceiptMatch()` — AUTO_LINK calls `prisma.unifiedTransaction.update` to set `expenseId` + `matchedDocumentId` on the matched row + writes a `TransactionEdit` with `editType=RECEIPT_LINK / source=AI`; NO_MATCH synthesises a new RECEIPT-sourced row on the user's Cash account; PICK_FROM returns candidates in `receiptMatch.candidates` for the future PR3.5 picker UI. **API:** new `POST /api/unified-transactions/cash` — 3-field body (amount + description + direction) with optional date + categoryLevel1; LOCKED-period guard (HTTP 423); SSOT bridge resolves the category through `CanonicalCategoryRegistry`; updates Cash account `currentBalance`. **UI:** new `<CashQuickAddButton />` (`components/bookkeeping/CashQuickAddButton.tsx`) — emerald floating action button bottom-right, modal with auto-focused tabular-nums amount field, in/out direction toggle, single-line "what was it for?", advanced (date + category) behind a `+` toggle, 700ms ✓ tick celebration → auto-close. Inline `<input type="file" capture="environment" accept="image/*">` for PWA camera path on mobile (file dialog fallback on desktop). Wired to `/dashboard/activity` page header strip. **Tests:** 32 new unit tests in `tests/bookkeeping/receiptMatcher.test.ts` (thresholds pinned at the spec values; daysBetween UTC-day-boundary; Levenshtein; vendorSimilarity normalisation; compositeScore weighting + strong-signal override; full scoreCandidate integration). **Cumulative: 72/72 green** (PR1 27 + PR2 13 + PR3 32). **BASIQ-on/BASIQ-off behaviour:** receipts that match existing QIF/CSV/manual rows auto-link via `applyReceiptMatch`; receipts that DON'T match land as RECEIPT-sourced rows on Cash. Future PR3.5 ships the reverse-direction matcher ("new bank tx → unmatched RECEIPT row → retro-link") so late BASIQ syncs reconcile cleanly. Build + typecheck clean (only pre-existing `stripe` module noise).

- **Phase 42 PR2 — Splits + Bulk Re-categorise + QIF wire-up + Categoriser SSOT bridge SHIPPED (this PR).** Second sub-PR of six. **Schema:** new `transaction_splits` table — hard FK to `canonical_category_registry` (the PR1 SSOT) for the per-line `categoryId`; per-line entity attribution (`propertyId` / `loanId` / `expenseId`); `isTaxDeductible` per split; cascade-delete with the parent `unified_transactions` row. Migration `20260510210000_phase_42_pr2_splits` — additive only (CREATE TABLE × 1, CREATE INDEX × 4, FK × 2; no row mutations; §12.11 N/A). **Service:** new `lib/bookkeeping/splits.ts` — single SSOT for the sum-validation rule (CLAUDE.md §12.3 — `assertSplitsBalance` with `SPLIT_SUM_EPSILON = $0.01`); `replaceSplits` (atomic delete-then-create inside a Prisma transaction; defends against cross-user FK abuse by verifying every `categoryId` belongs to the calling user; propagates the dominant split's category up to the parent transaction so all canonical engines — `getMasterFinancialSnapshot`, expense/income aggregators, reconciliation — keep working unchanged); `clearSplits`; `listSplits`; `resolveOrCreateCategoryForSplit`. Every mutation writes a `TransactionEdit` row with `editType=SPLIT` (fire-and-forget, deepEqual short-circuit). **QIF wire-up:** `lib/bank/types.ts:RawTransaction.splits` field + `lib/bank/parsers/qif.ts` propagation of `S`/`$` fields onto it (parser already extracted the data — PR2 wires it downstream); `app/api/unified-transactions/route.ts:handleBatchImport` resolves each split's category string via `resolveOrCreateCategory` (every category write goes through the SSOT registry; CLAUDE.md §12.2) and persists via `replaceSplits` with `source='IMPORT'`. **Categoriser SSOT bridge (PR1 carryover):** `app/api/unified-transactions/[id]/route.ts` PATCH now lazy-seeds `CanonicalCategoryRegistry` on every category write — closes the SSOT loop. **API:** new `GET/PUT/DELETE /api/unified-transactions/[id]/splits`; new atomic bulk endpoint `POST /api/unified-transactions/bulk-categorise` (max 200 per call, rolls up `MerchantMapping` learning rows in the same Prisma transaction, rejects the WHOLE batch if any row sits in a LOCKED period). **UI:** new `<BulkActionToolbar />` — sticky-bottom toolbar that surfaces when ≥1 row is selected; suggested-category chip strip + free-form custom-category input; mobile-first; `prefers-reduced-motion`-friendly. Activity `<TransactionRow />` adds a leading checkbox tap-target (rendered as a sibling element with `stopPropagation` so tapping the checkbox doesn't open the link dialog); selection persists across paginations until cleared or completed. **Tests:** 13 new unit tests across `tests/bookkeeping/{splits,qifSplits}.test.ts` — sum validation at the $0.01 boundary, empty-array rejection, mixed-sign tolerance, real-QIF-fragment parser parity. Cumulative: 40/40 green (PR1 27 + PR2 13). **BASIQ-on/BASIQ-off behaviour:** sum-validation runs identically regardless of source. BASIQ doesn't ship splits; users add them post-sync via the API. The inline split UI in `TransactionLinkDialog` is queued for PR2.5 (the dialog is 1,601 LOC; deferring keeps PR2 ship-able). Build + typecheck + tests clean (only pre-existing `stripe` module noise).
- **Phase 42 PR1 — Consumer Bookkeeping Foundation SHIPPED (PR #696).** First sub-PR of six in the XERO-complementary consumer bookkeeping stream (spec: `docs/blueprint/PHASE_42_CONSUMER_BOOKKEEPING_COMPLETION.md`). Foundational schema + service + audit + minimal UI hook. **Schema:** new `BookkeepingPeriodStatus` enum (OPEN/REVIEWED/LOCKED); three new tables `canonical_category_registry`, `bookkeeping_periods`, `transaction_edits` with the right unique + secondary indexes and FK-to-users cascade-delete; `unified_transactions.normalisedDescriptionHash` column added (nullable; backfilled deterministically for existing QIF/CSV/OFX rows); new partial index `ut_dedup_qif_csv_ofx` (non-unique at v1 — promotion to UNIQUE deferred to PR1.1 after a one-time prod audit per CLAUDE.md §12.11 caution). Migration `20260510200000_phase_42_pr1_foundation`. **Service helpers (`lib/bookkeeping/`):** `normaliseDescription.ts` — canonical normaliser + md5 hash, mirror of the migration backfill SQL, with full unit-test coverage; `categoryRegistry.ts` — `resolveOrCreateCategory()` lazy-seeds the SSOT registry on every categorisation call + `backfillRegistryForUser()` for one-time bulk seed jobs; `period.ts` — 3-status state machine with `getOrCreatePeriod`/`markPeriodReviewed`/`lockPeriod`/`unlockPeriod`/`isPeriodEditable`/`listPeriods`; `transactionEditAudit.ts` — fire-and-forget per-mutation audit with `pickCategoryFields`/`pickLinkFields` helpers and a deepEqual short-circuit (no row written when an edit didn't actually change a value). **API:** `GET/POST /api/bookkeeping/periods/[month]` (POST takes `{action: 'mark_reviewed'|'lock'|'unlock'}`); `[id]` PATCH on `unified_transactions` now refuses mutations on LOCKED periods (HTTP 423) and emits `TransactionEdit` rows for CATEGORY/TAGS/LINK_ENTITY/RECURRING mutations; new permissions `bookkeeping.read`/`bookkeeping.write` registered in `lib/auth/permissions.ts`. **UI:** new `<MonthlyReviewPill />` (`components/bookkeeping/MonthlyReviewPill.tsx`) on the Activity page header. Three visual states map to the three statuses; one-tap to mark reviewed; second-tap on LOCKED unlocks. Foundational hook for the PR6 Daily Pulse / streak / completion celebration. **Tests:** 27 unit tests across `tests/bookkeeping/{normaliseDescription,period,transactionEditAudit}.test.ts` — all green. **BASIQ-on/BASIQ-off behaviour:** identical. Registry resolves both BASIQ-fed and QIF-fed categorisations; dedup index is partial-on-source so BASIQ continues to use its own `basiqTransactionId` key. Tests verify BASIQ/MANUAL/BANK are NOT in DEDUP_SOURCES. **Out of PR1, queued:** category-resolver wiring into the categoriser (PR2's category-bridge); promotion of dedup index to UNIQUE (PR1.1 after prod audit). Build+typecheck clean except pre-existing `stripe` module noise.

### 2026-05-13
- **Phase 33g.4 — Consumer thread-status visibility + live polling SHIPPED (this PR).** Closes the next pair of consumer-side gaps from 33g.2/g.3: (a) when Reza marks a thread `SHIPPED` / `WONT_FIX` / `DUPLICATE` from `/admin/feedback`, the consumer drawer now shows a status chip in the thread-view header + a closed-state banner explaining what the status means + disables the composer (placeholder reads "This thread is closed. Start a new one to continue."). The status chip uses the same colour palette as the threads-list view (amber Open / sky In review / violet Planned / emerald Shipped / slate Won't fix + Duplicate). (b) While the drawer is in `thread` view, an 8-second polling loop pulls `GET /api/portal/feedback/[id]` and merges any new messages (admin replies, AI follow-ups) + reflects status changes — the consumer sees Reza's reply roughly within 8s of him sending it without needing to close + reopen the drawer. Polling skips while a submit is in-flight (avoids stepping on the existing `pollForAiReply` loop). Local optimistic message IDs (`local-*`) are recognised and not mistaken for missing-on-server. New `CLOSED_STATUSES` + `isThreadClosed()` + `CLOSED_MESSAGE` helpers in the drawer file. No backend / schema / vendor change. ~1 hr ship.

- **Phase 33g.2 — Live AI Feedback Chat SHIPPED (PR #730 merged).** Closes Up Next #39. Consumer dashboard chrome now carries a floating `<FeedbackButton />` (top-right, just left of `<HelpDrawerButton />` — same warm-ivory glass aesthetic) that opens a chat-style `<FeedbackChatDrawer />`. Two server-decided modes: AI-on (when `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is set in Vercel env, Claude Haiku 4.5 replies in real-time with clarifying questions, hard cap 6 AI turns/thread) vs AI-off (UI identical; no AI typing indicator; success message reassures the user with the 48hr SLA). New `respondToFeedbackThread()` in `feedbackService.ts` calls Anthropic fire-and-forget after every consumer/adviser reply; hard guards on (a) absent API key, (b) thread already handled by Reza (status PLANNED/SHIPPED/etc.), (c) AI-replying twice in a row, (d) 6-turn cap.
