### 2026-05-08
- **Phase 32C PR6a — Stripe test-mode subscription wiring SHIPPED (this PR).** Closes Up Next #18 at the demo-complete scope. The commercial mechanic is wired end-to-end for orgs subscribing to Studio / Practice plans; lead-fee invoice creation defers to PR6b. New `stripe@22.1.0` package. Three new Prisma models: `StripeCustomer` (one per Org, lazy-created when the owner first enters the billing flow; FK to Organization with cascade delete; `isTestMode` flag), `StripeSubscription` (per-Org subscription mirrored from webhook events; `planTier` is the new canonical `BillingPlanTier` enum STUDIO/PRACTICE/ENTERPRISE so no enum rename is needed — the legacy `OrganizationPlan` enum stays as the admin-set fallback shadow), `StripeWebhookEvent` (idempotency + audit log; unique constraint on `stripeEventId` dedupes Stripe re-deliveries; `processedAt` + `processingError` capture dispatch outcome). 7 new AuditAction values for the lifecycle (BILLING_CHECKOUT_STARTED / SUBSCRIPTION_CREATED / UPDATED / CANCELLED / INVOICE_PAID / INVOICE_FAILED / WEBHOOK_RECEIVED). Additive migration `20260508100000_add_stripe_billing` — no destructive writes, no row mutations. Canonical service `lib/services/stripeBillingService.ts` (~390 lines) with: `getOrCreateCustomer` (idempotent on organizationId; creates Stripe customer with `monitrax_organization_id` metadata for webhook-side lookup), `createCheckoutSession` (Studio/Practice via Stripe-hosted Checkout — caller redirects to `result.url`; success/cancel URLs configurable via env or per-call override; metadata propagated to subscription_data so the webhook handler always has stable lookup), `constructAndVerifyWebhookEvent` (Stripe signature verification at the route boundary — throws WEBHOOK_VERIFICATION_FAILED on mismatch), `handleWebhookEvent` (idempotent — dedupes via DB unique constraint, dispatches to subscription mirroring, records processing outcome on success or failure), `mirrorSubscription` (handles `customer.subscription.created/updated`; resolves planTier from metadata + price-id fallback; persists currentPeriodStart/End from the Stripe items array which is where the period now lives in 2025+ API), `markSubscriptionCancelled` (handles `customer.subscription.deleted`), `getSubscriptionStatus` / `getCustomerForOrg` (read helpers), `cancelAtPeriodEnd` / `resumeSubscription` (UI actions wired through to Stripe). Lazy client construction: `isStripeConfigured()` returns false when `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` is unset — billing UI renders friendly notice instead of crashing in dev/demo environments. Live-mode flag mirrored from Stripe onto every customer + subscription so the webhook handler routes to the right keyspace post-Basiq cutover. Plan-tier integration: new `resolvePlanTierForOrg(orgId)` in `lib/portal/planTier.ts` queries live StripeSubscription first (TRIALING/ACTIVE/PAST_DUE = entitled, honouring Stripe's 3-day grace window), falls through to legacy `OrganizationPortalSettings.plan` only when no subscription exists. This means feature gates now track billing state automatically. 5 new API routes: `GET /api/portal/billing` (returns subscription + customer + resolvedPlanTier + configured-flag for the UI), `POST /api/portal/billing/subscribe` (PORTAL_OWNER-only — mirrors marketplace listing submit guardrail; resolves owner email + creates Checkout session; returns `{ url }` for redirect), `POST /api/portal/billing/cancel` (sets cancel_at_period_end=true on Stripe), `POST /api/portal/billing/resume` (revokes scheduled cancellation), `POST /api/stripe/webhooks` (NO `withPermission` gate — Stripe is the caller and signature verification IS the auth mechanism; reads raw body via `request.text()` BEFORE parsing, per Stripe spec; 4xx on signature mismatch (Stripe retries on 5xx, not 4xx); 5xx on dispatch failure so Stripe retries with backoff; audit row for every received event). 1 new UI surface: `/portal/billing` page (Apple-glass aesthetic matching `PracticeGlassCard`) — current-plan card with status pill (TRIALING / ACTIVE / PAST_DUE / CANCELED colour-coded) + period-end date + test-mode badge + cancel/resume actions, three plan tiles (Studio AU$199 / Practice AU$599 with Recommended pill / Enterprise From AU$1,499 with mailto:sales@monitrax.com.au) with feature checklist + OWNER-only Subscribe button, `?success=true` / `?cancelled=true` URL-param banners on return from Checkout, NOT_CONFIGURED notice when Stripe keys unset (lists exact env vars to set), lead-fee billing footer card explaining PR6b is next. ZERO dependencies beyond `stripe` itself — billing UI uses existing PracticeGlassCard + `useOrganization` + `getPracticeProfessionConfig`. **Architecture decisions:** (1) BillingPlanTier is a NEW enum (STUDIO/PRACTICE/ENTERPRISE) rather than renaming the legacy `OrganizationPlan` enum (STARTER/PROFESSIONAL/BUSINESS/ENTERPRISE). Reason: enum rename in Postgres is `ALTER TYPE ... RENAME VALUE` which IS supported but BUSINESS doesn't have a clean target — the Xero-style 3-tier model collapses BUSINESS into PRACTICE which would be a destructive merge requiring §12.11 user-confirmation. The new enum on the new table sidesteps this entirely; the existing planTier.ts mapping shadow stays as the legacy fallback. Legacy enum rename remains queued for a future PR when no production rows depend on it. (2) Period start/end read from Stripe items array (`item.current_period_start`) not the subscription level (`sub.current_period_start`) — Stripe's 2025-09-30 API moved this; the change is forward-compatible since older API versions still expose subscription-level fields, which we fall back to via `(sub as any).current_period_start`. (3) Webhook handler dispatch failures throw inside the wrapped transaction so the StripeWebhookEvent.processingError column captures the message; the handler then re-throws to return 5xx so Stripe retries. (4) `resolvePlanTierForOrg` is async because it queries Prisma — callers (existing `withPortalFeatureGate` in `lib/auth/guards.ts`) will need a follow-up update to switch from sync `mapPlanToTier(legacyPlan)` to async `await resolvePlanTierForOrg(orgId)`; deliberately NOT done in this PR to keep scope tight (route-layer integration ships in PR6b alongside lead-fee invoicing). (5) ZERO new dependencies beyond `stripe` itself — no `@stripe/stripe-js` (Stripe-hosted Checkout means we don't render Stripe Elements client-side at v1; if we ever do that, the dep adds then). `npx tsc --noEmit` clean (exit 0); `npx next build` green; all 6 new routes registered (`/api/portal/billing`, `/api/portal/billing/subscribe`, `/api/portal/billing/cancel`, `/api/portal/billing/resume`, `/api/stripe/webhooks`, `/portal/billing`). Pitch playbook Step 7b populated end-to-end. **DEFERRED to PR6b (next slice):** lead-fee invoice creation hooked into PR4c `acceptRequest` (read `leadFeeChargedAt` column → create Stripe Invoice → record `stripeInvoiceId` on ProfessionalRequest); invoice history surface on `/portal/billing` showing both subscription invoices and lead-fee invoices in chronological order; webhook handlers for `invoice.paid` / `invoice.payment_failed` for lead-fee invoices specifically. **DEFERRED to PROD-ready:** live-mode key cutover, dunning logic (failed-payment grace + smart retry), refund flow, `OrganizationPlan` legacy enum rename (planTier.ts shadow handles the gap until then), receipt PDF generation, Stripe Customer Portal integration for self-service payment-method updates, Cloud Logging audit trail of every webhook event, dead-letter queue for repeatedly-failing events, Slack/PagerDuty alert on signature verification failure. **Phase 32C PR6b (lead-fee invoicing) + pitch fixture seed (#33) + pitch playbook final pass (#34) are now the only critical-path items left before demo-complete.**

Additive migration `20260507100000_add_conversations` — no destructive writes. Canonical service `lib/services/conversationService.ts` (~530 lines) with `createForAcceptedRequest` (idempotent; hooked into PR4c `acceptRequest` post-transaction so the welcome conversation lands the moment the adviser accepts, with the welcome member resolved from the responding-by-member-id) + `createOrgScopedConversation` (for the org-attached AskAPro picker → existing-pro path; idempotent on user-member pair so opening a conversation twice returns the same row) + `assertParticipant` (centralised access control — every read goes through this; cross-org leakage is structurally impossible because the API can't return a conversation without matching a ConversationParticipant row) + `listMessages` (with `since` cursor for poll loop + optional `markAsRead` for read-receipt update) + `postMessage` (size cap, role-frozen, outbound mirror fire-and-forget; channel=EMAIL_IN bypass for inbound webhook) + `softDeleteFromUserView` (consumer-only, hides without deleting — compliance archive persists) + `closeConversation` + `findConversationByReplyToSlug`. Email outbound: new `lib/email/conversationEmail.ts` — zero-dep SendGrid v3 send via fetch (no `@sendgrid/mail` package needed), activated by `SENDGRID_API_KEY` env var; when unset the function logs the would-be email + writes audit row + returns deterministic stub message id (dev/demo works without secrets, the architectural pattern is visible). Reply-to address format `monitrax+conv-<replyToSlug>@<inbound-domain>` so SendGrid Inbound Parse can route replies back to the right thread. Footer carries reply-to address + 7yr archive disclosure on every outbound. 5 new API routes: `GET/POST /api/conversations` (list user's + ensure-or-create org-scoped — caller-owns-link verification at the route boundary) + `GET/POST /api/conversations/[id]` (detail + softDelete/close actions) + `GET/POST /api/conversations/[id]/messages` (poll + send) + `POST /api/conversations/inbound` (SendGrid Inbound Parse webhook — extracts conversation slug from `to` header via regex, verifies sender-from matches consumer participant email, posts as EMAIL_IN with channel-bypass; v1 hardening = field validation + slug routing + 50KB body cap + sender-email match + quote-stripper heuristic; PROD hardening = DKIM/SPF strict mode, signed-event verification, sender-domain allowlist, Cloud DLP attachment scanning, rate-limiting per conversation, X-Monitrax-* tracing headers — all DEFERRED) + `GET /api/portal/conversations` (adviser inbox, org-scoped; active-membership check at the service layer). Existing surfaces wired in: PR4c request detail page (`/portal/requests/[id]`) now surfaces "Open conversation →" + "Open client view →" side-by-side on ACCEPTED requests via `getRequestForOrg` include + new `conversation: { id }` field; user-side `/dashboard/requests` accepted-state card surfaces "Open conversation →" deeplink via `listRequestsForUser` include; AskAPro picker MemberCard rewritten from `<Link href="/portal-message?memberId=...">` placeholder to `<button>` that calls new `POST /api/conversations` ensure-or-create endpoint with proper auth-gated flow + routes to `/dashboard/conversations/<id>` — leaky-funnel guardrail preserved at the endpoint (server verifies caller owns the org client link before creating). **Architecture decisions:** (1) Conversation creation runs AFTER PR4c accept transaction commits, not inside — `createForAcceptedRequest` has its own internal transaction; running it inside the accept transaction would either nest or break the consent invariant; running it after means a conversation-create failure does NOT roll back accept (the engagement is recorded in audit + ClientLink even if the conversation fails to create; ops can re-run create-conversation idempotently). - **Phase 41h.1 — AI Policy Gateway SHIPPED.** Reza brief 2026-05-06 reframed the Phase 41h.1 scope: instead of just a standalone validator, build a **centralised AI engine that defines the rules and guidelines for Gemini** so Gemini becomes a Monitrax-centric AI tool, not a generic one. General LLM smarts for paraphrase / education / structure remain available; numbers and citations are bound to Monitrax's calc engines. Industry-standard layered architecture under `lib/ai/tax-advisor/`: `gateway.ts` (single entry point — `createTaxAdvisorGateway()` factory, dependency-injected provider + auditSink + clock + traceId for testability) + `policy/systemPrompt.ts` (Monitrax persona; HR-1/HR-2/D-2 in prose; tool catalogue derived from registry) + `policy/responseSchema.ts` (Zod schema with 4 segment types — TEXT / NUMBER_REF / CITATION_REF / UNCOMPUTED_NOTE; tier discriminator TIER_1_FACTS vs TIER_2_ROUTE_TO_PRO with required `askAProRouting`) + `policy/validators.ts` (HR-1/HR-2/D-2 runtime post-processor with 6 issue codes; bare-financial-number regex catches `$1,250` / `27%` embedded in TEXT; recommendation-language regex catches "you should" / "I recommend" leak in TIER_1) + `providers/types.ts` (AIProvider interface + ProviderError typed exception + ProviderInvokeRequest/Response shapes) + `providers/mockProvider.ts` (deterministic provider for tests; queue-driven; supports tool-call dispatch + throwError for error-path tests) + `audit/auditLogger.ts` (CDR-safe AuditEntry — traceId / userId / provider / toolsInvoked / outcome / validationIssueCodes / durationMs / tokenUsage; explicitly excludes raw question + AI response text per CLAUDE.md §13.3; InMemoryAuditSink for tests + ConsoleAuditSink for dev; Cloud Logging wire-up lands in 41h.2). Gateway pipeline: trace-id + clock → buildSystemPrompt → empty ToolSession → provider.invoke (with executeTool callback that registers every call into session) → Zod shape validation → HR-1/HR-2/D-2 runtime validation against session → assemble citations + UNCOMPUTED + AFSL boundary footer via existing `renderBoundaryFootnote` → audit-write → return GatewayResponse. 5 status outcomes (OK / BLOCKED_VALIDATION / BLOCKED_RECOMMENDATION / PROVIDER_ERROR / SCHEMA_INVALID). Never throws to caller. 33 tests covering Zod schema (6) / HR-1 validator (6 — real path passes; unknown tool / fabricated path / bare $30k in TEXT / bare 50% in TEXT all rejected) / HR-2 validator (3 — real cit passes; fabricated cit-id / cit for un-invoked tool both rejected) / D-2 validator (3 — "you should" / "I recommend" rejected in Tier 1; same language allowed in Tier 2 routing) / UNCOMPUTED + Tier 2 routing (2 — fabricated UC flag rejected; Tier 2 missing routing rejected) / system prompt (3 — embeds HR-1/HR-2/D-2; lists every tool; explicitly forbids recommending) / gateway end-to-end with MockProvider (8 — OK; fabricated number BLOCKED_VALIDATION; fabricated citation BLOCKED_VALIDATION; "you should" BLOCKED_RECOMMENDATION; valid Tier 2 OK; SCHEMA_INVALID; PROVIDER_ERROR; unregistered-tool ProviderError) / audit logger (2 — records all metadata; CDR-safe — no raw question/response in entry). **505 total tests** (472 → 505, +33). tsc clean. Two configs shipped: `NSW_LAND_TAX_CY2025` (general $1.075M threshold + 3-bracket progressive scale to $88,036 + 2% over $6.571M; s5A special trust 1.5% × first $1.075M; Sch 1A 4% foreign on residential) + `VIC_LAND_TAX_FY2024_25` (general $50k threshold + 8-bracket progressive scale to $46,950 + 2.65% over $3M; s46IB trust 0.5% × value; s46IC absentee 4% on all taxable land). Order: general progressive → trust surcharge (DISCRETIONARY_TRUST + UNIT_TRUST_NON_FIXED only; UNIT_TRUST_FIXED exempt) → foreign / absentee surcharge. Helpers: `getLandTaxConfig(state)` (throws for unsupported QLD/SA/WA/TAS/ACT/NT — those land in 41e.13) + `getSupportedStates()`. 3 UNCOMPUTED flags every result: UC-MULTI-STATE-LAND-TAX (cross-state aggregation deferred to 41e.13) + UC-LAND-TAX-PPOR-EXEMPTION (caller's responsibility — pass `taxableLandValue: 0` if exempt) + UC-LAND-TAX-TRUST-SURCHARGE-NUANCE (v1 simplifies trust scale to flat %). Authority: NSW Land Tax Act 1956 (s10/s27/s5A/Sch 1A) + VIC Land Tax Act 2005 (Schedule 1/s46IB/s46IC). 33 module tests covering NSW general (6 brackets) + VIC general (4 brackets) + trust surcharge across ownership types (7) + foreign surcharge across resident-status (5) + combined NSW foreign discretionary trust @ $1.5M residential = $83,025 + UNCOMPUTED flag presence + citations + helpers. **503 total** (470 → 503, +33). tsc clean. Per going-forward commitment: `03_DATA_MODEL.md` new §10.24 + `PHASE_41_REGULATORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` §11.

New `lib/tax-engine/divisions/smsfTriumvirateClassifier.ts` covering: sole purpose test (s62 SIS) → 45% non-complying rate when fail (s295-160); in-house asset 5% cap (Pt 8 SIS) with BRP exception (s71(1)(b)); LRBA compliance (s67A + PCG 2016/5) with per-loan classification across 4 breach types + NALI_RISK; NALI at 45% on whole income amount per s295-550. Three constants exported: NON_COMPLYING_SMSF_RATE / NALI_RATE / IN_HOUSE_ASSET_CAP. Result reports per-regime status + applicableIncomeRate (15% or 45%) + anyBreach headline. 4 UNCOMPUTED flags (UC-SMSF-NON-COMPLYING / -IN-HOUSE-BREACH / -LRBA-BREACH / -NALI). 24 module tests covering all 4 regimes + BRP exception + per-loan LRBA paths + worst-case combination (all 4 flags surface) + edge cases. **470 total** (446 → 470, +24). tsc clean. Per going-forward commitment: `03_DATA_MODEL.md` new §10.23 + `PHASE_41_REGULATORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` §11.
- **Phase 41e.10 — FTE + IEE + 47% TFN withholding SHIPPED (PR #658).** New `lib/tax-engine/divisions/fteIeeClassifier.ts` per Sch 2F ITAA 1936. 4-outcome dispatch: INSIDE_FAMILY / INSIDE_VIA_IEE / OUTSIDE_FAMILY_FTDT (47% trustee tax) / TFN_WITHHOLDING (47% on non-quoting beneficiary). Family group test (s272-95) uses caller-asserted relationship. `FAMILY_TRUST_DISTRIBUTION_TAX_RATE` + `TFN_WITHHOLDING_RATE` constants exported (both 47%, indexed). Per-beneficiary result reports gross + FTDT + TFN withholding + net-to-beneficiary. UC-FTDT-OUTSIDE-FAMILY when FTDT applies; UC-FTE-CONTROL-TEST when CONTROLLED_ENTITY claimed. TFN withholding takes priority over OUTSIDE_FAMILY (no double-tax). 17 module tests; 446 total (429 → 446, +17); tsc clean. Per going-forward commitment: 03_DATA_MODEL.md new §10.22 + PHASE_41_REGULATORY_ARCHITECTURE.md §11.
- **Phase 41e.9 — Personal Services Income classifier SHIPPED (PR #657).** New `lib/tax-engine/divisions/psiClassifier.ts` with `classifyPsi(input)` per ITAA 1997 Part 2-42 + TR 2022/3. Five-test dispatch (80%-one-client / results / unrelated clients / employment / premises) with PSB Determination short-circuit. When NOT a PSB → full PSI attributed to individual at marginal rates per s86-15. Tests reported per-section for AFSL footer audit trail. UC-PSI-DEDUCTION-RESTRICTIONS surfaces for s86-60 deduction-restriction calc (deferred — caller computes net attributed amount). Not auto-wired into router (same pattern as Div 152 / 41e.8 — caller invokes directly when entity has PSI). 18 module tests covering all 5 tests + PSB Determination + 80% boundary cases + citation completeness. **429 total** (411 → 429, +18). tsc clean.

PERSONAL/SOLE_TRADER/PARTNERSHIP/SMSF offset losses against same-FY income (negative gearing); DISCRETIONARY_TRUST/UNIT_TRUST/COMPANY trap losses at entity level (carry forward, subject to 41e.15 loss-tests). Result shape carries `netResult` + `lossTreatment` + `lossAbsorbedThisFy` + `lossCarriedForward` + `taxableIncomeAtEntity` + `reason` + citations. UC-TAX-LOSS-CARRY-FORWARD when individual loss exceeds other income (multi-year tracking deferred). UC-TRUST-LOSS-TESTS / UC-COMPANY-LOSS-TESTS for trapped cases (full Sch 2F + Div 165 classifiers in 41e.15). Not auto-wired into router — caller invokes directly because input shape varies per loss source. 23 module tests covering all 7 entity types + 3 treatment paths + edge cases. **411 total** (388 → 411, +23). tsc clean. Per going-forward commitment: `03_DATA_MODEL.md` new §10.20 + `PHASE_41_REGULATORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` §11.
- **Phase 41e.7 — Div 152 small business CGT concessions SHIPPED (PR #655).** Stacks four concessions on top of Div 115: (1) 15-year exemption (s152-105 — entire gain disregarded if asset held ≥15yr + retirement/incapacity); (2) 50% active asset reduction (s152-205); (3) retirement exemption (s152-305 — $500k lifetime cap per s152-310); (4) small business rollover (s152-410 — defer remaining gain via replacement asset). Basic conditions test (s152-10): MNAV ≤ $6M (s152-15) OR turnover ≤ $2M (s152-20), AND asset is active (s152-35). New `lib/tax-engine/divisions/div152SmallBusinessConcessions.ts` with `applyDiv152(input)` returning per-concession `steps` array for AFSL audit trail. UC-DIV152-AGGREGATION flagged when MNAV/turnover near threshold (user must aggregate connected entities). UC-DIV152-RETIREMENT-CAP when lifetime cap binds. UC-DIV152-ROLLOVER for replacement-asset tracking (deferred). 24 module tests covering all four concessions + stacking interactions + edge cases. **388 total** (364 → 388, +24). tsc clean. Note: not auto-wired into router — Div 152 elections are financial-advisor decisions, caller invokes `applyDiv152()` directly when scenario-modelling. Per going-forward commitment: `03_DATA_MODEL.md` new §10.19 + `PHASE_41_REGULATORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` §11.

New `lib/tax-engine/divisions/div7aLoanClassifier.ts` with `calculateMinimumYearlyRepayment(B, N, r)` — standard amortising-annuity for s109N — and `classifyDiv7ALoans(loans)` for COMPLIANT / MRP_SHORTFALL / NO_AGREEMENT / SUB_TRUST_UPE dispatch. Wired into `entityTaxRouter` for COMPANY entities — when `EntityTaxFacts.div7aLoans` is provided, `result.div7aClassification` carries the full result + Div 7A / s109D / s109N / s109Y citations. Income tax dispatch (base-rate 25%/30%) still UNCOMPUTED — that's 41e.7 territory. **"Never false silence" pattern preserved** — COMPANY now reports Div 7A exposure paired with `UC-ENTITY-COMPANY` for the income-tax dispatch. UC-DIV7A-DISTRIBUTABLE-SURPLUS surfaces when total deemed dividend > 0 (s109Y surplus cap deferred). - **Phase 41e.0 → 41e.5 stack integration + s100A zone classifier SHIPPED (this PR).** Two things bundled: (1) integration of the previously-stranded 41e.0 → 41e.4 chain that merged into each other's branches but never reached main (PRs #634-#652 lived on a chain rooted at `claude/phase-41e1-c-trust-distribution`); (2) net-new 41e.5 — s100A reimbursement-agreement zone classifier. The classifier replaces the always-on `UC-S100A-RISK` flag from 41e.1 slice C with a real per-beneficiary WHITE/GREEN/BLUE/RED zone classification per ATO PCG 2022/2: RED for explicit reimbursement-scheme signals (UPE + funds not received OR funds used by another OR minor + funds-not-received); WHITE for testamentary trusts (PCG 2022/2 ¶13); GREEN for FTE family-group distributions where the beneficiary materially benefits; BLUE default. Conservative by design — only confidently classifies WHITE/GREEN/RED when input data carries strong signals. New `s100aFacts` + `isTestamentaryTrust` fields on `EntityTaxFacts.trustDistribution` (and the underlying `TrustDistributionInput`); new `s100aClassification` on `TrustDistributionResult`. POST /api/tax/entity/[id] accepts the new body fields. UC-S100A-RISK flag REPLACED by UC-S100A-NUANCED (only surfaces when classification is BLUE or RED). 23 new tests (20 classifier + 3 router-integration). **344 tests total** (324 → 344). tsc clean. Per the going-forward commitment: `03_DATA_MODEL.md` new §10.17 + `PHASE_41_REGULATORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` §11 status flip.
- **Phase 33g — Adviser Feedback Inbox (async) SHIPPED (this PR).** Closes Up Next #35; closes Open Question Q-FB-1 by Claude judgement under Reza directive *"use your judgement and continue."* End-to-end async feedback channel for post-pitch + pilot advisers: write side at `/portal/feedback` (master-detail with new-thread form auto-filling `surfaceRoute` from `?route=` query param), read side at `/admin/feedback` (Reza inbox with status filter chips, status flipper, internal-notes textarea, tag-for-AI checkbox, two Markdown export buttons). Schema (additive only — no destructive write per §12.11): 4 new enums (`FeedbackSurfaceTag` BUG/FEATURE/UX/PRAISE/QUESTION/COMPLIANCE × `FeedbackSeverity` LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH × `FeedbackStatus` OPEN/IN_REVIEW/PLANNED/SHIPPED/WONT_FIX/DUPLICATE × `FeedbackAuthorRole` ADVISER/MONITRAX_ADMIN), 2 new tables (`feedback_threads` with `internalNotes` + `taggedForAi` + `lastReviewedAt`, `feedback_messages` cascading from threadId), 3 new `AuditAction` enum values via additive `ALTER TYPE ... ADD VALUE IF NOT EXISTS` (`FEEDBACK_THREAD_REPLIED` / `FEEDBACK_THREAD_STATUS_CHANGED` / `FEEDBACK_INTERNAL_NOTE_UPDATED`). Migration `prisma/migrations/20260505180000_add_feedback_inbox/migration.sql`. Service: `lib/services/feedbackService.ts` (~340 LOC) is the canonical CRUD + audit boundary — `createThread`, `appendReply` (auto-flips OPEN → IN_REVIEW on first admin reply), `updateAdminFields` (audits each field on every change), `listAdviserThreads`/`getAdviserThread` (sanitises `internalNotes` out before return), `listAdminThreads`/`getAdminThread`, `exportThreadsAsMarkdown` (server-side excludes `internalNotes`; respects `taggedForAi` + `since` filters). `notifyAdviserOfReply()` is a documented swap-point that fires no-op v1 — Phase 32C PR4d swaps in SendGrid. Permissions: `feedback.read` + `feedback.write` registered in `lib/auth/permissions.ts` (every role gets both — feedback is not gated; locking it behind OWNER would be hostile UX). API: 4 adviser routes (`GET/POST /api/portal/feedback`, `GET /api/portal/feedback/[id]`, `POST /api/portal/feedback/[id]/reply`) all `withPermission`-gated; 4 admin routes (`GET /api/admin/feedback`, `GET/PATCH /api/admin/feedback/[id]`, `POST /api/admin/feedback/[id]/reply`, `GET /api/admin/feedback/export?since=&taggedOnly=`) all `verifyAdminGCPAuth`-gated with `mfaSetupRequiredResponse()` enforcement per CLAUDE.md §13.4. Architecture decision documented in `lib/services/feedbackService.ts` file-header JSDoc: keep `FeedbackThread` separate from Phase 32C PR4d's `ProfessionalConversation` — different participants (Monitrax↔adviser vs adviser↔client), different retention rules (24mo default vs 7yr archive), different status workflow (bug-tracker shape vs message-only); consolidating would create a discriminator nightmare per §12.2 SSOT (logged here so a future session doesn't try to merge them). Five §9 design-doc questions resolved by Claude: naming = "Send feedback" verbs / "Feedback" nouns; email = in-app v1 with documented swap-point; severity = default `MEDIUM`; internal-notes audit = every edit (body of note NEVER logged — only previousLength + nextLength); SLA = 48 hours stated in form copy (cron-based 48h flagging deferred to follow-up). Integrations: help-drawer footer gets "Send feedback →" link (only surfaces for `org-*` audience sets) with `?route=<pathname>` pre-fill; `PracticeHeader` gets a "Send feedback" pill in the action slot; portal sidebar adds Feedback to secondary nav with chat-bubble icon; admin sidebar adds "Adviser Feedback" under Operations. Pitch playbook unchanged (the help drawer + PracticeHeader integrations already make the channel discoverable during the demo). Typecheck clean against the available module set; remaining errors are pre-existing missing-module noise that resolves on `npm install`. Vercel preview will run `prisma migrate deploy` against `monitrax-db-dev` — green build proves the migration applies cleanly + the 4 enums + 2 tables + 3 audit enum extensions land additively.
- **Phase 41e.−1 cleanup PR D — archetype fixtures + master-config self-test + parity baselines SHIPPED (PR #633 merged).** Final slice of the four-slice Phase 41e.−1 cleanup PR. After PRs A (#626 — config extension + FY25-26), B (#629 — consumer migration, resolves C-2 + H-1 through H-6), C (#630 — `buildTaxSummary()` regression-trap fix, resolves C-1), this slice closes 41e.−1 by capturing pre-refactor baselines for the next 17 sub-PRs. Adds `tests/tax-engine/fixtures/archetypes/{sarah-kim,david-emma,olivia}.ts` — three persona fixtures shared with pitch seeding (audit doc §9.1 + Up Next #33): Sarah Kim (sole-trader IT consultant + Pty Ltd, $130k salary in 30% bracket); David + Emma Mitchell (family with discretionary trust + corporate-trustee SMSF, salary + rental + franked dividends); Olivia Novak (multi-entity HNW with 4 properties + trust + unit trust + Pty Ltd + SMSF, $300k+ income in 45% bracket). Adds `tests/tax-engine/config/taxYearConfig.test.ts` — the **master-config self-test** per audit §10.4: asserts every required field is populated on every FY (FY23-24, FY24-25, FY25-26); brackets cover [0, ∞) with no gaps or overlaps; bracket rates are non-negative and ascending; `bringForwardThresholds` are monotonically ordered (full ≤ reduced ≤ none); `superContributionsTaxRate` locked at the canonical 15%; `reviewSchedule.nextReviewBy` for the current FY is in the future (forces explicit human review before each FY rolls in — overdue reviews fail CI). Adds `tests/tax-engine/parity/buildTaxSummary.parity.test.ts` — the **slice-C parity baseline** asserting `calculateTaxPosition()` runs cleanly against each archetype, returns non-negative tax fields, marginal rate is one of the canonical bracket rates (percentage scale 0–100, not decimal — caught + fixed a real bug from slice C where `marginalTaxRate` was double-multiplied to 3000), refund matches `paygWithheld − netTax` within $1 rounding, deductions sum equals category breakdown, and each archetype lands in the expected bracket band. **Caught a real bug:** the parity test for Olivia (45% bracket assertion) flagged that `IncomeTaxResult.marginalRate` is already in percentage scale (0-100, multiplied by `* 100` at `incomeTaxCalculator.ts:112`) — slice C's `(result.tax.marginalRate ?? 0) * 100` was double-converting, producing 3000 instead of 30 in the master snapshot. Fixed in this PR by changing the conversion to a pass-through. **Validates the audit's snapshot-test protocol** (§9.2) — captured a regression that integration testing wouldn't have surfaced cleanly. 40 new tests, all passing. Full unit suite: 172 passed (132 existing + 40 new). Audit doc status flipped to "✅ AUDIT COMPLETE + 🟡 41e.−1 cleanup in flight (slice D = this PR)". After this PR merges, **41e.−1 is COMPLETE** and **41e.0 (foundation: types + entity-aware aggregator extensions + cycle-detection + permissions + new endpoints) is unblocked**. tsc clean.

Resolution date validated against FY (s207-58 + s115-228 require resolution by 30 June). New `BeneficiaryDistribution.character` field reporting the per-beneficiary breakdown across franked / cgt / ordinary. Five FY-end states pinned: no pools / pools no streaming / valid streaming (flag off) / invalid resolution / streaming without pools. 6 new tests. **324 tests passing** (318 → 324). POST endpoint validates + passes streaming body through. After 41e.4, the v1 caveat *"Tax allocated proportionally across entities; exact Div 6/6E trust distribution math lands with Phase 41e"* on the Money Flow Sankey is now resolvable — slice that wires Sankey to per-entity streaming dispatch is queued.

SMSF dispatch result extended to `{ capResult, highIncomeSuperTax }`. Pre-existing SMSF tests updated. - **Phase 41e.1 slice D-2 — wire CGT into `entityTaxRouter` SHIPPED (PR #650). Closes 41e.1.** New optional `EntityTaxFacts.cgtEvents` + `carryForwardCapitalLosses` + `smsfIsComplying` + `isForeignResident` fields. New optional `EntityTaxPosition.cgtResult` independent of `result` so a COMPANY entity (income tax UNCOMPUTED — that's 41e.7 territory) can carry a fully-computed CGT figure with **0% discount per s115-280**. For TRUST entities with BOTH `trustDistribution` AND `cgtEvents`, the router populates BOTH `result` (Div 6 distribution) AND `cgtResult` (Div 115 net capital gain) with deduplicated cumulative citations. POST `/api/tax/entity/[entityId]` extended to accept `cgtEvents` + `carryForwardCapitalLosses` body fields. **First demonstration of the audit's "never false silence" complement to "never false numbers"** — when an entity's income-tax dispatch is still UNCOMPUTED, the CGT side calc still surfaces with full citations + UNCOMPUTED-flag transparency. 8 new router tests pinning the new dispatch contract: PERSONAL_NAME with cgtEvents → 50% discount applied; COMPANY with cgtEvents → cgtResult populated + result still null (income tax UNCOMPUTED); SMSF complying → 33⅓%; TRUST with both inputs → both populated independently with deduplicated citations; losses > gains → carry-forward residual; prior-year losses applied via body field; no cgtEvents → cgtResult undefined; empty array → undefined. **303 tests total passing** (295 → 303). tsc clean. Per the going-forward commitment: `03_DATA_MODEL.md` §10.13 slice D-2 row, `PHASE_41_REGULATORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` §11 status flip ("**41e.1 COMPLETE**"), `07_API_STANDARDS.md` §15 (POST endpoint row updated to mention D-2 fields). **Phase 41e.2 (SMSF contribution caps) starts next.**
- **Phase 41e.1 slice D-1 — wire `trustDistribution` into `entityTaxRouter` SHIPPED (PR #649).** First user-testable Div 6 surface. Extends `EntityTaxFacts` with optional `trustDistribution` field (`trustNetIncome` + `beneficiaries[]` + `hasFamilyTrustElection?`). When provided for DISCRETIONARY_TRUST or UNIT_TRUST entities, the router runs `allocateTrustDistribution` (slice C) and produces a real `EntityTaxPosition.result` with per-beneficiary breakdown + s95/s97 citations + the always-on UC-S100A-RISK / UC-DIV-6E-STREAMING flags. Without distribution data, trust entities still flag UNCOMPUTED — backward-compatible with slice 41e.0 D's existing GET endpoint. New `entityHasConditionalComputedTax(type)` helper exposes the conditional capability matrix. New **`POST /api/tax/entity/[entityId]`** handler accepts a JSON body with `trustDistribution` so the wiring is testable via curl until a UI captures distribution data: `curl -X POST -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" -d '{"trustDistribution":{"trustNetIncome":100000,"beneficiaries":[{"id":"b1","name":"Sarah","presentlyEntitledShare":1.0}],"hasFamilyTrustElection":true}}' /api/tax/entity/<trustId>` returns the computed Div 6 allocation with citations. 6 new router tests covering: TRUST without data → UNCOMPUTED, TRUST with data → computed, UNIT_TRUST with data → pro-rata, partial entitlement → s99A penalty, COMPANY/SMSF still UNCOMPUTED, PERSONAL_NAME ignores trustDistribution. **295 tests total passing** (289 → 295). tsc clean. Per the going-forward commitment: `03_DATA_MODEL.md` §10.13 slice D-1 row, `PHASE_41_REGULATORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` §11 status, `07_API_STANDARDS.md` §15 (new POST endpoint row) all updated in the same PR. Slice D-2 (CGT events into router) is the final 41e.1 slice.
- **Phase 41e.1 trust distribution slice C SHIPPED (PR #647).** New `lib/tax-engine/divisions/trustDistribution.ts` exporting `allocateTrustDistribution(input)` + `getDistributableAmount(result)` + `S99A_TRUSTEE_PENALTY_RATE` constant. Implements **ITAA 1936 s95** (net income definition), **s97** (presently-entitled beneficiary assessment at marginal rate), **s99A** (trustee penalty rate 47% on undistributed residual when no beneficiary is presently entitled). Validates beneficiary shares — throws on negative or sum > 1.0 (deed over-distribution) with floating-point tolerance to allow `1/3 + 1/3 + 1/3`. Always flags `UC-S100A-RISK` (full reimbursement-agreement zone classifier per TR 2022/4 + PCG 2022/2 lands in 41e.5; rationale wording differs for FTE vs non-FTE trusts) and `UC-DIV-6E-STREAMING` (character allocation lands in 41e.4 — v1 distributes franking + capital gains generically pro-rata to net-income share, not streamed to specific beneficiaries). Flags `UC-S98-TRUSTEE-ASSESSMENT` when any beneficiary is non-resident or under legal disability. 20 unit tests covering basic distribution (single + 2-way + 3-way), s99A penalty (zero entitlement / partial entitlement), validation errors, all UNCOMPUTED flag triggers, citation rules (s99A appears only when residual > 0), helper functions, edge cases (zero income, zero with beneficiaries). **289 tests total passing** (269 → 289). tsc clean. Per the going-forward commitment, all relevant docs updated in this same PR: `03_DATA_MODEL.md` §10.13 slice C row, `PHASE_41_REGULATORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` §11 status callout. Slice D (router wiring — first user-visible 41e.1 surface) is next.
- **Phase 41e.1 capital loss netting slice B SHIPPED (PR #645).** New `lib/tax-engine/divisions/capitalLossNetting.ts` exporting `applyCapitalLossNetting(input)`. Implements **ITAA 1997 s100-50** (loss-method ordering: prior-year + current-year losses applied to gains; FIFO carry-forward residual), **ITAA 1997 s115-100** (discount applied to NET gain after losses, NOT to gross gains then summed — the consumer-tax mistake explicitly caught by a dedicated test), **ITAA 1997 Div 102-A** (assessable net capital gain). Composes slice A's `calculateCgtDiscount` per entity. When losses exceed gains, surfaces the unconsumed residual via `carryForwardOut` for the next FY's CGT register. Mixed holding periods raise `UC-CGT-MIXED-HOLDING` with proportional discount applied to the qualifying-share. 18 unit tests including the critical **s115-100 ordering pin**: gain $100k + loss $30k under 50% discount = $35k assessable, not $20k. **269 tests total passing** (251 → 269). tsc clean. Per the going-forward commitment, all relevant docs updated in this same PR: `03_DATA_MODEL.md` §10.13 slice B row, `PHASE_41_REGULATORY_ARCHITECTURE.md` §11 status callout updated.
- **Phase 41e.1 Div 115 CGT discount slice A — entity-aware rate dispatch SHIPPED (PR #644).** First **rule** sub-PR after the 41e.0 foundation. Pure additive: new module `lib/tax-engine/divisions/cgtDiscount.ts` exporting `calculateCgtDiscount(input)` + `getCgtDiscountRate(entityType, isComplying?)`. Per-entity rates: PERSONAL_NAME / SOLE_TRADER / DISCRETIONARY_TRUST / UNIT_TRUST / PARTNERSHIP → **50%** (ITAA 1997 s115-25); SMSF complying → **33⅓%** (ITAA 1997 s115-100); SMSF non-complying → **0%** (s115-100 carve-out); COMPANY → **0%** (ITAA 1997 s115-280 explicitly cited in result so the boundary footer can show exactly why); any entity < 12 months held → 0% (s115-25 universal gate). Foreign-resident raises an `UC-FOREIGN-RESIDENT-CGT` UncomputedFlag pointing at Subdiv 115-D apportionment as a future sub-PR rather than computing the proportional rule (the calc still returns the standard discount; UNCOMPUTED is surfaced for the user). 24 unit tests in `tests/tax-engine/divisions/cgtDiscount.test.ts` covering every entity branch + the holding-period boundary (exactly 12 months → discount applies; 11 months → no discount) + SMSF complying/non-complying split + foreign-resident UNCOMPUTED + edge cases (zero gain, negative gain trusted to caller). No consumers wired yet — slice D of 41e.1 will replace the COMPANY / DISCRETIONARY_TRUST UNCOMPUTED branches in `entityTaxRouter` to call this for CGT-event inputs. **251 tests total passing** (227 → 251, +24). tsc clean. - **Phase 41e.0 foundation slice A — permissions + entity-aware orchestration types SHIPPED (PR #634).** First slice of 41e.0. Pure additive type contract; zero consumer changes; zero behaviour changes; sets up the contract for slices B/C/D + 41e.1 → 41e.17. Two new permissions in `lib/auth/permissions.ts` — `tax_data.read` (mirrors `report.read` audience for VIEWER+) gating per-entity tax position queries, `tax_data.write` (CONTRIBUTOR+) gating computational mutations like trust-distribution composition or CGT-disposal calc. Two intentional deviations from architecture doc pseudocode: `EntityTaxPosition.result` typed `unknown` (sub-PRs refine without churn); `EntityTaxFacts.incomes/expenses/depreciations` inlined as structural rows rather than `import`-from-position to avoid circular imports. Both documented inline.
- **Phase 41e.−1 cleanup PR D — archetype fixtures + master-config self-test + parity baselines SHIPPED (this PR).** Final slice of the four-slice Phase 41e.−1 cleanup PR. After PRs A (#626 — config extension + FY25-26), B (#629 — consumer migration, resolves C-2 + H-1 through H-6), C (#630 — `buildTaxSummary()` regression-trap fix, resolves C-1), this slice closes 41e.−1 by capturing pre-refactor baselines for the next 17 sub-PRs. Adds `tests/tax-engine/fixtures/archetypes/{sarah-kim,david-emma,olivia}.ts` — three persona fixtures shared with pitch seeding (audit doc §9.1 + Up Next #33): Sarah Kim (sole-trader IT consultant + Pty Ltd, $130k salary in 30% bracket); David + Emma Mitchell (family with discretionary trust + corporate-trustee SMSF, salary + rental + franked dividends); Olivia Novak (multi-entity HNW with 4 properties + trust + unit trust + Pty Ltd + SMSF, $300k+ income in 45% bracket). Adds `tests/tax-engine/config/taxYearConfig.test.ts` — the **master-config self-test** per audit §10.4: asserts every required field is populated on every FY (FY23-24, FY24-25, FY25-26); brackets cover [0, ∞) with no gaps or overlaps; bracket rates are non-negative and ascending; `bringForwardThresholds` are monotonically ordered (full ≤ reduced ≤ none); `superContributionsTaxRate` locked at the canonical 15%; `reviewSchedule.nextReviewBy` for the current FY is in the future (forces explicit human review before each FY rolls in — overdue reviews fail CI). Adds `tests/tax-engine/parity/buildTaxSummary.parity.test.ts` — the **slice-C parity baseline** asserting `calculateTaxPosition()` runs cleanly against each archetype, returns non-negative tax fields, marginal rate is one of the canonical bracket rates (percentage scale 0–100, not decimal — caught + fixed a real bug from slice C where `marginalTaxRate` was double-multiplied to 3000), refund matches `paygWithheld − netTax` within $1 rounding, deductions sum equals category breakdown, and each archetype lands in the expected bracket band. **Caught a real bug:** the parity test for Olivia (45% bracket assertion) flagged that `IncomeTaxResult.marginalRate` is already in percentage scale (0-100, multiplied by `* 100` at `incomeTaxCalculator.ts:112`) — slice C's `(result.tax.marginalRate ?? 0) * 100` was double-converting, producing 3000 instead of 30 in the master snapshot. Fixed in this PR by changing the conversion to a pass-through. **Validates the audit's snapshot-test protocol** (§9.2) — captured a regression that integration testing wouldn't have surfaced cleanly. 40 new tests, all passing. Full unit suite: 172 passed (132 existing + 40 new). Audit doc status flipped to "✅ AUDIT COMPLETE + 🟡 41e.−1 cleanup in flight (slice D = this PR)". After this PR merges, **41e.−1 is COMPLETE** and **41e.0 (foundation: types + entity-aware aggregator extensions + cycle-detection + permissions + new endpoints) is unblocked**. tsc clean.
- **Phase 41e.−1 cleanup PR C — buildTaxSummary delegation SHIPPED (PR #630 merged).** Resolves audit C-1 — the headline regression trap. `buildTaxSummary()` no longer reimplements brackets inline at `masterFinancialService.ts:1012-1077`; it now delegates to `calculateTaxPosition()` from the canonical Phase 20 tax engine and adapts the result back into the legacy `TaxSummary` shape. Behaviour change is intentional + documented per audit §9.2 capture-before-refactor protocol: the new code includes Medicare Levy (Health Insurance Levy Act 1982) + LITO/SAPTO/franking/foreign offsets (ITAA 1997 Div 126-H/126-L/207/770) + franking gross-up + categorised deductions, none of which the old inline code applied. Numbers now match `/api/tax/position` for the same user. Consumer shape preserved — `TaxSummary` interface unchanged; every consumer (dashboard, AI advisor, Sankey) continues working without modification. Adapter pattern is intentional and stays until 41e.0+ swaps consumers directly onto `TaxPositionResult`.

H-6 ($3k/property depreciation) renamed to `HEURISTIC_PROPERTY_DEPRECIATION_PER_YEAR` and flagged as `UC-PROPERTY-DEPRECIATION` in audit §10.3.** Behaviour-preserving: every replaced hard-code returns the same numeric value via the new config path on FY24-25 (slice A populated FY24-25 with values matching what consumers were hard-coding). Grep audit confirms no remaining `* 0.85`, `* 0.15`, `27500`, `60400`, `250000` literals.

- **Phase 32C PR4a — Professional Marketplace SHIPPED (this PR).** Closes Up Next #13. First Phase 32C deliverable on the Demo-Complete Critical Path, ships in parallel with Session D's Phase 41 build (zero territory overlap — marketplace owns `ProfessionalListing` + `ProfessionalRating`, entity layer owns `LegalEntity`). Schema: two new Prisma models + four supporting enums (`ListingStatus`, `ProfessionalSpecialisation` × 15 values, `ListingTargetTier` × 4 values, `ListingRegion` × 12 values) + five new AuditAction enum values for the lifecycle (UPDATED / SUBMITTED / APPROVED / REJECTED / SUSPENDED). Matching additive migration `prisma/migrations/20260505140000_add_professional_marketplace/migration.sql` — no destructive writes, no row touches, §12.11 checklist N/A. Service `lib/services/marketplaceService.ts` (~440 lines) consolidates Org-side / admin-side / public scopes into one canonical service; one engine, scope is a parameter, not a fork. Submit-time validation: ≥10-char tagline, ≥100-char blurb, ≥1 specialisation + region, discipline-conditional compliance number (AFSL for FINANCIAL_ADVISOR, credit rep for MORTGAGE_BROKER, TPB for TAX_AGENT/ACCOUNTING_FIRM); editing APPROVED → flips back to PENDING_REVIEW (admin re-checks before re-publish); editing REJECTED → returns to DRAFT. Permissions: portal `marketplace:listing:read|write|submit` (read = all roles incl. VIEWER; write = OWNER+ADMIN; submit = OWNER only — anti-poaching guardrail mirroring `team:invite`); admin `marketplace:listings:read|approve|reject|suspend` (SUPER_ADMIN full; SUPPORT_ADMIN + VIEWER read-only). Three new UI surfaces — (1) `/portal/marketplace/listing` Org-side editor with `MarketplaceListingEditor` client component (Apple-glass aesthetic matching `PracticeGlassCard`, status-aware form locking when PENDING_REVIEW, discipline-conditional compliance fields, accessible checkbox groups for facets, sticky bottom action bar, REJECTED/SUSPENDED feedback panels with admin reasoning shown inline, auto-derived publicSlug until user explicitly types one); (2) `/admin/marketplace/listings` admin queue (defaults to PENDING_REVIEW filter, status + discipline dropdowns, full-text search, click-to-drill into `/admin/marketplace/listings/[id]` detail page with one-tap deeplinks to ASIC moneysmart register / TPB public register / ABR for the AFSL / credit rep / TPB / ABN respectively, manual cross-check checkboxes that record `asicCrossCheckedAt` + `tpbCrossCheckedAt` for the auditor trail, free-text `verificationNotes` scratchpad, approve/reject/suspend action buttons with required reason text); (3) `/marketplace` public browse (D2C discovery surface with hero copy, search + discipline + region + specialisation filters, listing cards with rating + accepted-request count + specialisation chips + region preview, sorted by averageRating desc + acceptedRequestsCount desc + alpha) and `/marketplace/[slug]` public detail (full blurb, specialisations as chips, target-tier "best fit for" list, regions, recent ratings, "Connect" CTA pointing at `/signup?intent=connect&listing=<slug>` — Phase 32C PR4b's `<AskAProfessionalButton>` plugs in here). Public surfaces have a compliance footer explaining the ASIC + TPB cross-check + AFSL/credit/TPB licence boundary on every page. **Architecture decision: zero new dependencies.** Recharts (already in deps from Phase 41d) is reused only via existing imports; no new packages added. Listings are invite-only at v1 — Monitrax-curated 10-20 launch professionals; the admin approve/reject flow is the gate. **Architecture decision: lead-fee tiers stored per-listing.** Per-Org overrides at admin-approval time (rare; defaults stand for most listings) — surface is in the admin detail page if needed. Pitch playbook Step 6 placeholder populated. `npx tsc --noEmit` clean (exit 0). `npx next build` green with all 11 marketplace routes registered (`/admin/marketplace/listings`, `/admin/marketplace/listings/[id]`, `/api/admin/marketplace/listings`, `/api/admin/marketplace/listings/[id]`, `/api/marketplace/listings`, `/api/marketplace/listings/[slug]`, `/api/portal/organizations/[orgId]/marketplace-listing`, `/api/portal/organizations/[orgId]/marketplace-listing/submit`, `/marketplace`, `/marketplace/[slug]`, `/portal/marketplace/listing`). **DEFERRED to PROD-ready:** automated ASIC/TPB API cross-check (manual at v1), Cloud Storage for hero images (placeholder field stored), email notifications on approve/reject (admin reaches out manually at v1). - **Phase 41g — Adviser drill-in entity layer SHIPPED (this PR).** `/portal/clients/[id]/view` extended with a 3-tab toggle: **Structure** (default — the Phase 41c `EntityTree` populated with the CLIENT's data) | **Money Flow** (the Phase 41d `MoneyFlowSankey`) | **Dashboard** (the existing `ClientCanonicalDashboard` from 32B PR3). The adviser sees the entity tree FIRST when opening a client view — per the brief, *"the adviser cannot give wealth advice without seeing the structure first; this surfaces it prominently."* Two new portal endpoints — `GET /api/portal/clients/[id]/entities` (returns `{ entities, members }` for the client) and `GET /api/portal/clients/[id]/money-flow` (returns the per-entity flow shape) — both delegate to the same canonical services (`listEntitiesForUser`, `getMoneyFlow`) but pass the client's userId, not the caller's. New shared helper `lib/portal/adviserClientAccess.ts` extracts the consent + membership + role + assignment guard from the existing `/snapshot` route so all three portal endpoints share one canonical access check; the snapshot route was refactored to use it (reviewers reject any new portal client-data endpoint that doesn't route through `verifyAdviserClientAccess`). Audit: piggybacks on the page-level `PRO_DASHBOARD_VIEW` row already written by the snapshot endpoint — no per-component log spam (if component-level access logs are ever required for compliance, add new action codes like `PRO_ENTITY_VIEW` and emit them at the route layer). **Read-only in adviser view** — advisers cannot edit a client's entity layer (no AddCTA, no EditDialog wiring; tile clicks are no-ops). Future Phase 41 slice may surface a "Suggest a structural change" affordance that opens an Ask-a-Pro thread, but that's a separate workstream. Drill-in page now fetches snapshot + entities + flow in parallel; entities/flow failures don't block the Dashboard tab (the tree handles zero entities, Sankey handles `isEmpty`). Pitch playbook Step 2/3 updated to reflect that the entity tree is reachable from the same drill-in URL — no tab switching, no separate page. **Phase 41h (AI entity-aware diagnosis) and 41f (Xero/MYOB integration) are now the only Phase 41 surfaces left for demo-complete pre-pitch.** tsc clean.
- **Phase 41d — Money Flow Sankey at `/dashboard/entities` SHIPPED (PR #617 merged 2026-05-05).** Demo-Complete Critical Path Wk-6/7 deliverable + the **second wow moment** in the lighthouse pitch (Step 4). 3-stage visualisation: **Income sources** (Salary / Rental / Investment / Other) → **Legal entities** (role-coloured, matching the Phase 41c tree palette so the two surfaces share visual language) → **Outflows** (Tax / Essential expenses / Discretionary / Loan repayments / Surplus). New service `lib/services/moneyFlowService.ts` exporting `getMoneyFlow(userId)` — pulls Income / Expense / Loan rows in parallel, classifies income by source label (SALARY → Salary; RENTAL/RENT → Rental; INVESTMENT → Investment; everything else → Other), aggregates expenses by entity × essential/discretionary, computes loan repayments per entity from `minRepayment` annualised, allocates tax (PAYG withholding) proportionally to each entity's share of taxable income, and computes surplus as the residual. SSOT: uses canonical `toAnnual` from `lib/utils/frequencies.ts`; reads raw rows directly so per-entity attribution stays accurate (the master snapshot's `incomeBreakdown` aggregates across entities). New API route `app/api/money-flow/route.ts` — thin wrapper, `withPermission('report.read')` (same gate as `/api/master-snapshot`). New presentational component `components/entities/MoneyFlowSankey.tsx` rendered with **`recharts <Sankey>`** (already in deps — `@nivo/sankey` and `d3-sankey` evaluated and rejected per CLAUDE.md §12.7+§12.8: zero new dependencies). Custom Node renderer outputs role-coloured Apple-glass tiles (PERSONAL warm amber → OPERATING emerald → HOLDING indigo → SUPERANNUATION violet → INVESTMENT fuchsia, matching the 41c tree palette) with AUD labels outside the column. Custom Tooltip shows `Source → Target $X per year`. Headline-summary chip strip above the canvas (Income / Tax / Essentials / Discretionary / Loans / Surplus or Deficit) so the viewer reads the totals before the flows. **v1 tax allocation is proportional, not Div 6/6E-correct** — flagged inline as an italic caveat below the canvas: *"Annual reference period. Tax allocated proportionally across entities; exact Div 6/6E trust distribution math lands with Phase 41e."* Honest, audit-friendly. Page-level changes: new tab toggle on `/dashboard/entities` (Structure | Money Flow); tab state lifted to the page; lazy-fetch when the tab is activated; cache invalidates on entity mutation (add/edit/remove from Structure tab). Same Bearer-token auth pattern as the entities API call. Empty state: friendly "Not enough data to draw your money flow yet" hero when the user has no income or no expenses. `prefers-reduced-motion` honoured (entrance fade collapses). Phase 41e (full entity-aware tax engine — replaces the proportional v1 allocation with Div 6/6E + s100A + Div 7A correctness) and 41h (AI entity-aware diagnosis composing the same flow shape) unblocked. tsc clean.

### 2026-05-04
- **Phase 41c — Interactive Entity Tree at `/dashboard/entities` SHIPPED (this PR).** Reza directive 2026-05-04: *"Yes it should replace, the tree view should be an interactive and live tree view that user can view, track and navigate through the tree view."* Replaces the 41b list view with a 3-row glass-tile tree (People → Legal Entities → owned-objects chips inline), SVG Bézier connectors between rows, dashed fuchsia paths for trustee→trust corporate hierarchy via the `parentEntityId` self-FK. New `components/entities/EntityTree.tsx` (~520 lines) and shared `components/entities/types.ts` (Entity / HouseholdMember / OwnedObjectsCount / `ROLE_PALETTE` per-role colour table). Page-level changes: parallel fetch of `/api/household-members` so the People row reflects real household composition (with a "You" anchor fallback when no members exist); list-rendering block (98 lines) replaced with a single `<EntityTree>` call (5 lines); entity-tile remove buttons retired in favour of a Remove affordance in the `EntityFormDialog` footer (canonical entry point now: tile click → edit dialog → either Save or Remove). Role palette: PERSONAL warm amber / OPERATING emerald / HOLDING indigo / SUPERANNUATION violet / INVESTMENT fuchsia — colour-blind-readable with text labels. Owned-objects rendered as in-tile chips (clickable, drill to `/dashboard/properties` / `/dashboard/balances` / `/dashboard/investments/accounts` / `/dashboard/assets`). Mobile fallback (<md): suppresses SVG, stacks tiles vertically, surfaces "trustee: …" labels for corporate hierarchy. `prefers-reduced-motion` honoured throughout (motion lines render but don't animate). Built with framer-motion (already in deps); zero new dependencies — `reactflow` / `@xyflow/react` evaluated and rejected per CLAUDE.md §12.7+§12.8 (custom code is simpler than a 150kb dep for a 3-row tree). v1 People→Entity edge heuristic: PERSONAL_NAME entities match by case-insensitive name to a HouseholdMember; non-PERSONAL_NAME entities fan out from every PERSONAL_NAME entity's matched person (the "principals"). Phase 41e will replace this with proper shareholder/beneficiary fields once those land in the schema. Page header chip flipped from `Sparkles` to `TreePine` to match the new metaphor. - **Phase 33c (light) — Per-article Save-as-PDF SHIPPED (PR #613).** Closes Up Next #21 (light scope; ZIP bundle deferred to PROD). Demo-Complete Critical Path week-13 deliverable — completes the in-app help triplet (33a Help Center + 33b drawer + 33c PDF) so every help article in the demo env is auditor-downloadable. Three new files: `app/print/help/[...slug]/page.tsx` (sibling top-level print route — bypasses `app/help/layout.tsx` chrome so the print body is genuinely clean, no CSS hide-the-nav hacks; uses the SAME `lib/help/markdown.ts` zero-dep renderer the screen view uses; A4-targeted print CSS with `@page 18mm/16mm/22mm/16mm` margins, page-break-inside: avoid on `<pre>` / blockquote / list items, page-break-after: avoid on headings, emerald link colour preserved in print; document header carries Monitrax brand + audience + reviewed date; document footer carries canonical URL provenance which is what auditors expect from a compliance pack), `app/print/help/[...slug]/layout.tsx` (tiny white-bg wrapper with `robots: { index: false, follow: false }` metadata so search engines don't see the print pages), `app/print/help/[...slug]/AutoPrint.tsx` (small client component — waits for `document.fonts.ready` then triggers `window.print()` on next animation frame so the user lands on the page with the Save-as-PDF dialog already open; re-triggerable via the toolbar "Open print dialog" link so a dismissed dialog isn't a dead-end; 80ms paint-buffer delay so the toolbar paints before the modal). One file edit: `app/help/[...slug]/page.tsx` gains a "Download as PDF" link in the article header (`print:hidden` so it doesn't leak into print, target="_blank" + rel="noopener", inline 16×16 download chevron SVG, opens `/print/help/<audience>/<articleSlug>`). Static-generated for every article in `generateStaticParams()` — 8 paths in this build (3 consumer + 1 org-admin + 1 org-professional + 5 compliance). `npx next build` green; `npx tsc --noEmit` clean. **Architecture decision: zero new dependencies.** Evaluated `@react-pdf/renderer` — 1,005 transitive packages with 36 vulnerabilities including 2 critical / 9 high. Rejected per CLAUDE.md §12.7 (managed-service / minimum-custom-code) — the browser-native print-to-PDF pipeline is what Stripe Docs / MDN / GitHub use for the same reason: deterministic, accessible, respects user paper-size + orientation preferences, and the resulting PDF stays selectable + searchable. Compliance auditors care about evidence + provenance, not pixel polish. **Architecture decision: print route OUTSIDE `/help/*`.** Initial attempt placed it at `app/help/[...slug]/print/page.tsx`; Next.js rejected this with "Catch-all must be the last part of the URL." Pivoted to `/print/help/<audience>/<slug>` — sibling top-level route — which is structurally cleaner anyway: the print view is a genuinely different surface (clean document, no chrome) and a top-level `/print/...` prefix is a natural home for future Phase 33f DOCX templates. **Pitch playbook Step 8** — compliance pack table updated to mention the per-article download capability so the lighthouse adviser sees it during the meeting. Reza directive: parallel work to Session D's Phase 41 build — zero territory overlap (Phase 41 owns entity layer, Phase 33c owns help system).
- **Phase 41b — Onboarding wizard "How is your wealth held?" + standalone entity management SHIPPED (PR #612).** Wk-3 deliverable on the Demo-Complete Critical Path. Three new surfaces + three new API routes + one new service slice + one new validators module, all tied together by the Phase 41a foundation. (1) **Wizard step** at `components/onboarding/wizard/steps/EntitiesStep.tsx`, slotted between Household and Properties so subsequent steps can attach to entities. Captures type (LegalEntityType), role (LegalEntityRole), warm display name, optional ABN (with mod-89 checksum validation), optional ACN (with ASIC complement checksum), optional opt-in TFN (encrypted via `lib/security/tfnEncryption.ts` per CLAUDE.md §13 — never logged, never sent to AI, never displayed in plaintext after entry), trustee→trust parent link via wizard-local `parentEntityTempId`. Smart default: empty entity list lets bulk-create fall through to `getDefaultLegalEntityId()` and attach everything to the user's PERSONAL_NAME entity (idempotent with the 41a backfill). Behaviour-psychology copy throughout — "most Australian wealth-builders hold their assets across more than one legal entity" (normalising) not "you should have set up a structure" (shaming). (2) **Two-pass writer** in `app/api/onboarding/bulk-create/route.ts` — pass 1 inserts all entities without parent FK, pass 2 maps wizard temp-ids to real DB ids and wires up trustee→trust corporate links. New `wizardEntityMap` in scope for future wizard steps to attach owned rows to non-default entities. (3) **Standalone surface** at `app/dashboard/entities/page.tsx` — "My Structure" page added to TRACK-stage sidebar as a child of My Accounts per CLAUDE.md §14.2 (entities are foundational to "what you own"). Cards list every entity with type/role/ABN/ACN/parent + owned-objects summary; add/edit dialog mirrors the wizard step's UX; remove flow uses AlertDialog with the destructive-remove guard's friendly counts surfaced inline ("This entity owns 3 properties, 2 loans..."). (4) **API routes** — new `app/api/entities/route.ts` (GET list / POST create) and `app/api/entities/[id]/route.ts` (GET / PUT / DELETE). All behind canonical `withPermission('entity.read|write|delete')` (new perms added to `lib/auth/permissions.ts` mirroring property/loan rows). All state-changing routes write `logCRUD` audit rows with `sanitizeCdrMetadata`-wrapped payloads — TFN value NEVER appears in metadata, only `hasTfn: boolean`. DELETE returns 409 Conflict with structured `counts` object when the entity has owned objects (instead of a raw P2003 FK violation), plus a hard "can't remove last PERSONAL_NAME entity" guard so the default-owner fallback always exists. PUT/DELETE use `withPermission<RouteContext>('perm', async (request, auth, context) => { const { id } = await context!.params; })` per the loans route pattern. (5) **Service extension** at `lib/services/legalEntityService.ts` — adds `listEntitiesForUser` (returns `LegalEntitySummary[]` with `_count` aggregation across all 7 owned-row tables; never returns `tfnEncrypted` value, only `hasTfn: boolean`), `createEntity` / `updateEntity` (encryptTfn-wrapped, parent-ownership-validated), `deleteEntity` (throws `EntityHasOwnedObjectsError` with the friendly counts before the FK guard would fire). Re-exports added to `lib/services/index.ts`. (6) **AU validators** — new canonical module `lib/utils/auValidators.ts`: `isValidAbn` (11-digit modulus-89 per ATO ABN spec), `isValidAcn` (9-digit ASIC complement checksum per ASIC RG 22), `isValidTfnFormat` (8/9-digit format check; checksum is not publicly available per ATO security-by-obscurity policy), plus `formatAbn` (`XX XXX XXX XXX` ATO display) and `formatAcn` (`XXX XXX XXX` ASIC display). Used by both client-side wizard validation and server-side route validation — single SSOT. (7) **AIHelper** entities-step content added so the wizard's contextual help button works on the new step. tsc clean. Phase 41c (Entity Tree visualisation) unblocked — next session decision: replace list at `/dashboard/entities` with the tree view, or keep both surfaces (list = management, tree at sub-route = visualisation). Pitch playbook Step 3 entity-seed sub-bullets populated for all 3 archetypes (Sarah Kim Pty Ltd; Mei Family Trust + corporate trustee + SMSF; Olivia's full multi-entity stack with Discretionary + Unit + Pty Ltd + SMSF).

- **Phase 41a — `LegalEntity` schema + ownership backfill SHIPPED (this PR).** Foundation for the entity layer that everything in Phase 41b–h depends on. New Prisma model `LegalEntity` (uuid pk, userId FK cascade, name, type [PERSONAL_NAME / COMPANY / DISCRETIONARY_TRUST / UNIT_TRUST / SMSF / PARTNERSHIP / SOLE_TRADER], role [PERSONAL / HOLDING / OPERATING / INVESTMENT / SUPERANNUATION], abn?, acn?, tfnEncrypted?, tradingName?, establishedDate?, self-FK parentEntityId? for trustee→trust hierarchies, indexes on userId + type + parentEntityId). Two new enums `LegalEntityType` + `LegalEntityRole`. Added `ownerEntityId` (NOT NULL after backfill, FK with `ON DELETE RESTRICT`) to seven owned models — `Property`, `Loan`, `Account`, `InvestmentAccount`, `Asset`, `Income`, `Expense`. Matching migration `prisma/migrations/20260504130000_add_legal_entity/migration.sql` runs additively in five steps: (1) CREATE TYPE for both enums; (2) CREATE TABLE legal_entities + self-FK + user-FK + 3 indexes; (3) ADD COLUMN ownerEntityId NULLABLE on each owned table; (4) backfill — INSERT one PERSONAL_NAME LegalEntity per existing user via `gen_random_uuid()` named after `users.name`, then UPDATE every owned row WHERE `ownerEntityId IS NULL` to point at its user's PERSONAL_NAME entity (§12.11 IS-NULL guard ensures we only touch rows the migration just added the column to); (5) ALTER each ownerEntityId to NOT NULL + index + FK. New canonical service `lib/services/legalEntityService.ts` exporting `getDefaultLegalEntityId(userId, [tx])` — resolves the user's PERSONAL_NAME entity (creating one on demand for brand-new registrations between 41a deploy and 41b ship); accepts an optional transaction client so callers inside `prisma.$transaction` can compose. Eighteen call sites updated to provide `ownerEntityId` on every owned-row create: `app/api/properties|loans|accounts|assets|income|expenses|expenses/bulk|investments/accounts|onboarding/bulk-create|accounts/[id]/import|bank/import|basiq/sync|documents/analyze/confirm|recurring-payments/[id]/link|transactions/[id]/link/route.ts`, `lib/bank/recurringExpenseDetection.ts`, `lib/testing/loader.ts`, `prisma/seed-validation.ts`. TFN at-rest helper `lib/security/tfnEncryption.ts` mirrors the `MFAMethod.secret` Phase 10 pattern — `encryptTfn` (8/9-digit validation + base64 wrap), `decryptTfn` (digit-shape revalidation), `maskTfn` (display safe `***-***-XYZ`); single swap-point for KMS-backed CMEK upgrade when Up Next #3 lands. CLAUDE.md §13 hard rules enforced in code: `tfnEncrypted` is optional + default-off, never logged, never in audit metadata, never sent to AI. Doc sync complete: `docs/architecture/03_DATA_MODEL.md` extended with new §10 (Entity Layer — why an entity layer, the LegalEntity shape, the ownerEntityId pattern, migration & backfill, default-entity service, TFN handling, what 41b–h enables); `docs/pitch/LIGHTHOUSE_ADVISER_PITCH.md` Step 3 "Open the entity tree (THE moment)" placeholder populated with entity-tree visual reference + which 3-archetype seeded users best showcase the visual; this row's Up Next entry collapsed to ~~25~~ ✅ SHIPPED; Up Next #26 (Phase 41b) flipped to "UNBLOCKED — next session". `npx tsc --noEmit` clean. ## How to update this document (rules)

> **Mandatory.** See CLAUDE.md §15 (Implementation Plan Protocol) for full rule text.

1. **Every PR that starts a workstream** adds it to `🟡 Active Workstreams` with all the fields filled in.
2. **Every PR that advances a workstream** ticks off the relevant `[ ]` checkbox(es) in that workstream's phase list and updates `Last touched`.
3. **Every PR that completes a workstream** moves it from `🟡 Active` to `✅ Recently Completed` with the date and PR number.
4. **Every PR that surfaces a tech-debt item** (e.g. removes a duplicate, identifies dead code) adds it to `🗑️ Dead Code / Tech Debt Backlog`.
5. **Every PR that reverts a previous attempt** adds an entry to `↩️ Reversed Decisions` so the same dead-end isn't re-attempted in a future session.
6. **Every PR that introduces an open question** the user hasn't decided adds it to `❓ Open Questions`.
7. **Once a quarter** (or when this file exceeds ~600 lines), recently-completed items older than 30 days are moved to `IMPLEMENTATION_CHANGELOG.md` and removed from this file.
8. **Reviewers reject PRs** that materially change a workstream without updating this file. Same hygiene rule as the changelog.
